Result: LLM相比人工修复倾向于修改更多行代码，部分情况下直接生成全新解；即使错误提交已接近人工补丁，允许LLM从零生成时的解题正确率仍高于在其基础上打补丁。

Conclusion: LLM的调试行为更偏向替换而非增量修复。这对AI辅助编程工具设计有重要启示：应支持用户调试过程，鼓励渐进式问题求解策略，而非直接替换解法。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that Large Language Models can effectively solve problems and fix bugs in diverse programming environments, including competitive programming. Existing approaches primarily evaluate LLM performance in problem solving or bug fixing independently, but do not explore the relationship between these two capabilities. This work focuses on determining how much the LLM deviates from a buggy solution to fix the bug compared to a human-written patch, and if there is a bias towards generating entirely new solutions. We construct a dataset with all the submissions ($\sim$ 3000) from a couple of users from Codeforces, and we match each buggy submission with its corresponding human fix. By using the similarity between the buggy solution and the human fix as a baseline, we evaluate the quality of LLM-generated bug fixes on 3 OpenAI GPT models (gpt-5-nano, gpt-5-mini, gpt-5.1). We check if the generated solutions solve the problem by using the Codeforces-R1 dataset, an openly available dataset that has tests generated with the DeepSeek-R1 model. Our findings suggest that LLMs tend to modify more lines than necessary compared to human fixes and, in some cases, generate entirely new solutions. We also observe that LLMs solve more problems correctly when allowed to generate solutions from scratch rather than patch buggy submissions, even when those submissions are close to the human patch. This has important implications for the design of AI-assisted programming tools, particularly in supporting user debugging processes and promoting incremental problem-solving strategies rather than solution replacement.

</details>


### [44] [Controlling Backchannels in Streamable Full-duplex Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29418)
*Maike Züfle,Peter Polák,Sefik Emre Eskimez,Jan Niehues,Peter Bell,Ondřej Klejch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出轻量级"反馈头"(backchannel head)，从全双工对话模型的隐藏状态预测反馈词起始时机，阈值触发强制解码，在7B与1B模型上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈词(backchannel，如"uh-huh")是自然对话的核心要素，但现有全双工语音对话模型很少对其显式建模。

Method: 设计一个轻量级反馈头，基于全双工模型自身的隐藏状态预测反馈词应何时开始；当该概率超过可调阈值时，强制解码生成反馈词。将该模块分别接入7B(PersonaPlex)与1B(F-Actor)模型以验证跨规模泛化性。

Result: 探针实验证实隐藏状态能预判真实人类反馈时机；生成评估显示反馈词更频繁且时机更准确；人类评分者认为所生成反馈词与真实反馈词相当。

Conclusion: 显式建模反馈词时机是一种轻量且有效的方案，可跨模型规模泛化，并使生成反馈在频率、时机与自然度上接近人类水平。

Abstract: Backchannels, brief acknowledgements like "uh-huh" produced while the other party may still be talking, are central to natural conversation, but full-duplex spoken dialogue models rarely model them explicitly. We introduce a lightweight backchannel head that predicts, from a full-duplex model's own hidden states, when a backchannel should begin. Once this probability crosses a tunable threshold, a backchannel is force-decoded. Attached to both a 7B (PersonaPlex) and a 1B (F-Actor) model, it generalizes across scale. Probing confirms the hidden states anticipate real human timing, and generation evaluation shows more frequent, better-timed backchannels. Human raters judge the resulting backchannels on par with real ones.

</details>


### [45] [Rufus-Air: An Open LLM Post-Training Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29421)
*Chia-Yuan Chang,Renyuan Cheng,Rui Feng,Xiaotian Han,Yuan He,Hongye Jin,Linwei Li,Shiyang Li,Fenglin Liu,Xin Liu,Priyanka Nigam,Haoyang Wen,Zhenghao Xu,Zhuocheng Xu,Bing Yin,Qingyu Yin,Chao Zhang,Rongzhi Zhang,Zhihan Zhang,Zixuan Zhang,Zixuan Zhang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Rufus-Air 是一个基于 GLM-4.5-Air-Base（106B-A12B）的开放、可复现后训练配方，采用八阶段串行流程，仅用开源组件与公开数据，性能超越官方后训练版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练（post-training）流程往往缺乏透明性与可复现性，数据、奖励设计、基础设施与阶段顺序等关键要素鲜有完整记录，且常依赖人工标注或内部蒸馏教师模型。本文旨在提供一套完全开放、可复现且不依赖新人工标注的后训练配方。

Method: 在 GLM-4.5-Air-Base（106B-A12B）上进行八阶段串行后训练流水线：SFT → Reasoning RL → Coding RL → Instruction-Following RL → General Agent → Coding Agent → Search Agent → RLHF。完整记录数据、奖励设计、基础设施、阶段顺序及各阶段结果；阶段由基础能力向高级能力推进，奖励信号由硬性可验证奖励向较软的基于评判模型（judge-based）的信号过渡。训练基于开源组件与公开数据，大部分数据按原发布状态使用，不引入新人工标注，也不使用内部蒸馏教师。

Result: Rufus-Air 在性能上优于官方 GLM-4.5-Air 后训练版本，并与同等规模的开放模型具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 主要发现有四点：(i) 多样且高质量的 SFT 为能力奠定了坚实基础下限；(ii) 难度过滤使 RL 提示保持在有效的学习区间内；(iii) 奖励可靠性为阶段排序提供了实用原则；(iv) 基础设施与工程选择是配方的一部分，而非单纯的实现细节。

Abstract: Rufus-Air is an open and reproducible post-training recipe on GLM-4.5-Air-Base (106B-A12B), organized as a serial pipeline of eight stages: SFT, Reasoning RL, Coding RL, Instruction-Following RL, General Agent, Coding Agent, Search Agent, and RLHF. We document the data, reward design, infrastructure, stage order, and stagewise results needed to reproduce the recipe. Stages progress from basic to advanced capabilities and from hard, verifiable rewards to softer judge-based signals. Training builds on open-source components and public data, much of it used as released, without new human annotation or an in-house distillation teacher. Our main findings are that (i) diverse, high-quality SFT establishes a strong capability floor; (ii) difficulty filtering keeps RL prompts within a productive learning range; (iii) reward reliability provides a practical principle for ordering stages; and (iv) infrastructure and engineering choices are part of the recipe, not just an implementation detail. Rufus-Air improves over the official GLM-4.5-Air post-trained release and is competitive with similarly sized open models.

</details>


### [46] [agentic-ger: terminology recovery in long-form speech using global context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29428)
*Yanqiao Zhu,Wupeng Wang,Zhifu Gao,Xiangang Li,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM智能体的Agentic-GER框架，利用全局上下文和选择性重转录纠正长语音中的领域术语转录错误，在中英文上一致提升术语准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长语音ASR虽随语音语言模型进步而改善，但领域特定术语的转录仍难以准确、稳定。受LLM世界知识与上下文能力启发，希望借助LLM智能体解决术语纠错问题。

Method: Agentic-GER以LLM为智能体：利用完整转录的全局上下文识别可疑术语、消解歧义假设；选择性对源语音重新转录以验证候选修正；将接受的编辑用于指导后续决策，形成迭代纠错流程。

Result: 在GigaSpeechBench上，使用四种LLM与两种ASR系统实验，中英文（含thinking与非thinking模式）术语表现均一致提升；中文语音上相比Whisper基线，biased character error rate（B-CER）相对降低最多36.8%。

Conclusion: LLM智能体式术语纠正能有效提升长语音ASR的术语转录质量，具备跨模型、跨语言与跨ASR系统的鲁棒性和一致性。

Abstract: Recent advances in speech language models have improved automatic speech recognition (ASR) for long-form audio. However, accurately and consistently transcribing domain-specific terminology remains challenging. Motivated by the world knowledge and contextual capability of large language models (LLMs), we propose Agentic-GER, an LLM-based agent for terminology correction in long-form speech. The agent uses global context from the full transcript to identify suspicious terms and resolve ambiguous hypotheses. It selectively re-transcribes the source speech to check candidate corrections, and uses accepted edits to guide subsequent decisions. Experiments with four LLMs and two ASR systems on GigaSpeechBench show consistent terminology improvements in both Chinese and English, with and without thinking. On Chinese speech, Agentic-GER achieves up to a 36.8% relative reduction in biased character error rate (B-CER) over the Whisper baseline.

</details>


### [47] [IterSynth: Rethinking Deep Search Agents via Role-Decoupled Iterative Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29444)
*Xingyu Wu,Yuchen Yan,Zhengxi Lu,Siqi Chen,Xin ZHANG,Aiting Liu,Chao Deng,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Jian Shao,Jun Xiao,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出角色解耦与摘要驱动的 IterSynth 范式及 RDPO 强化学习算法，缓解 ReAct 式智能体的角色耦合与上下文累积问题，在多项深度搜索基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ReAct 式智能体在深度搜索中面临两大局限：一是角色耦合，单一策略需同时承担规划、证据利用与综合生成；二是上下文累积，搜索历史不断增长引入噪声并掩盖有用信息。

Method: 提出 IterSynth，一种角色解耦、基于摘要的范式，在负责识别信息需求的 Planner 与将证据整合进演化摘要状态的 Synthesizer 之间交替；摘要作为搜索的持久状态。并引入 Role-Decoupled Policy Optimization（RDPO），结合终端结果奖励与轮级评分表评估，计算角色特定优势以实现更精确的信用分配。

Result: 在 BrowseComp、Xbench-DS 等五个长时程深度搜索基准上，IterSynth-8B 平均得分 50.7，超过最强 ≤8B 先前智能体 +4.2%。作为模型无关的提示范式，在前沿专有模型上相较 ReAct 等提示范式带来显著零样本提升。

Conclusion: 角色解耦与摘要机制可有效降低能力耦合与上下文噪声，RDPO 提供更精确的信用分配；IterSynth 既可作为高效训练框架，也可作为通用提示范式，在深度搜索任务上具有广泛适用性与优越性能。

Abstract: Deep search requires LLM agents to decompose complex queries, search for evidence, and synthesize grounded answers, yet existing ReAct-style agents suffer from two limitations: role coupling, where one policy must handle planning, evidence use, and synthesis; and context accumulation, where growing search histories introduce noise and obscure useful information. To address these issues, we propose IterSynth, a role-decoupled and summary-based paradigm that alternates between a Planner for identifying information needs and a Synthesizer for integrating evidence into an evolving summary state. This design separates planning from synthesis while using the summary as the persistent state of search, reducing both capability coupling and context noise. To train IterSynth effectively, we further introduce Role-Decoupled Policy Optimization (RDPO) for reinforcement learning, which combines terminal outcome rewards with turn-level rubric evaluations and computes role-specific advantages for more precise credit assignment. Experiments on five long-horizon deep-search benchmarks such as BrowseComp and Xbench-DS show that IterSynth-8B achieves an average score of 50.7, surpassing the strongest prior $\leq$8B agent by +4.2\%. Moreover, IterSynth serves as a model-agnostic prompting paradigm, delivering substantial zero-shot gains over ReAct and similar prompting paradigms on frontier proprietary models.

</details>


### [48] [Two Emojis of Difference: What Multilingual Affective Generation Benchmarks Actually Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29445)
*Fardeen Sadab,Adib Sakhawat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对多语言emoji摘要生成基准的审计表明，其结论多是测量工具（标注者差异、输出长度、归一化单位、数据划分）的伪影而非系统真实属性；作者提出"emoji-affect decodability"参考式探针，排名跨随机种子稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感生成基准（如emoji摘要评测）以偏好评分排名系统，但作者怀疑这些排名结论由测量方法本身（标注者、长度、归一化、划分方式）造成，而非模型能力差异，因此需要系统审计并寻找更稳健的评测方式。

Method: 用8个指令微调LLM为17,100条孟加拉语、英语和印地语句子生成emoji摘要，收集6,960条人工评分；将标注者作为随机因子而非固定因子做方差分析；考察标注者方差贡献、留一标注者敏感性、输出长度与系统方差关系及长度匹配比较（2,599对）；检验跨提供方各向异性对均值中心化的敏感性、每语言token成本随归一化单位的变化、多视图按行划分对macro-F1的影响；提出基于参考的"emoji-affect decodability"探针。

Result: 将标注者视为随机因子时，任意系统间均无显著差异（F(7,14)=0.59, p=0.76），而传统分析却判定28组两两比较中19组显著；标注者身份解释的评分方差远高于系统身份，去掉任一标注者都会改变最优系统；平均emoji数量解释系统间方差的78.7%，长度匹配比较（2,599对）可逆转排行榜；跨提供方各向异性差异在均值中心化后消失，每语言token成本随归一化单位变号，多视图按行划分使macro-F1虚高3.1个百分点并改变排名第一的系统；所提探针排名跨种子稳定在±0.003 macro-F1。

Conclusion: 该多语言情感生成基准的主流结论是测量工具的伪影而非系统属性，偏好评分类评测不可靠；应放弃偏好评分，改用对标注者、长度、归一化与划分稳健的"emoji-affect decodability"参考式探针。

Abstract: We audit a multilingual affective generation benchmark eight instruction-tuned LLMs producing emoji summaries for 17,100 Bangla, English and Hindi sentences, with 6,960 human judgements and find its headline conclusions to be artefacts of the measurement instrument rather than properties of the systems. Treating annotators as a random rather than a fixed factor, no system differs significantly from any other ($F(7,14)=0.59$, $p=0.76$), although the conventional analysis declares 19 of 28 pairwise differences significant. Annotator identity explains far more rating variance than system identity, and the winning system changes whenever any single annotator is removed. The ordering that does emerge tracks output length: mean emoji count explains 78.7\% of between-system variance, and a within-item length-matched comparison over 2,599 pairs reverses the leaderboard. We further show that cross-provider anisotropy differences vanish under mean-centring, that per-language token costs change sign with the normalising unit, and that multi-view row-wise splits inflate macro-F1 by $3.1$ points and change the top-ranked system. In place of preference scoring we propose **emoji-affect decodability**, a reference-based probe whose rankings are stable to $\pm0.003$ macro-F1 across seeds.

</details>


### [49] [YODAS v3: Over 1 Million Hours of High-Bandwidth, Stereophonic, Multilingual Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29448)
*William Chen,Shinnosuke Takamichi,Sayaka Shiota,Satoru Fukayama,Samuele Cornell,Shinji Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: YODAS v3 是一个包含 147 种语言、超 110 万小时 48kHz 多声道音频的弱标注语音语料库，是目前最大的开放语音数据集，也是首个大规模高保真立体声语音语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放语音数据集在规模、语言覆盖或音频保真度方面存在不足，缺乏同时具备超大规模与高保真立体声的语料库，因此需要构建更全面的语音资源以支撑多语言语音识别与神经编解码研究。

Method: 提出语料库采集方法，引入用于收集语言均衡语音数据的新技术；对数据组成进行广泛分析，包括语言分布、音频质量与转写质量；并训练基线语音识别与神经编解码模型以验证数据集有效性。

Result: 语料库覆盖 147 种语言，其中 22 种语言数据超过 1 万小时，73 种语言超过 5 千小时，语言分布体现了采集方法的有效性；基线模型实验验证了数据集的有效性。

Conclusion: YODAS v3 以 CC BY 3.0 许可发布，在规模、语言覆盖与高保真立体声音频方面具有显著优势，可作为多语言语音识别与神经编解码研究的重要基础资源。

Abstract: We present YODAS v3, a weakly-labeled speech corpus containing over 1.1 million hours of 48kHz multi-channel audio in 147 languages, released under a CC BY 3.0 license. YODAS v3 is not only the largest open speech dataset to date, but also the first truly large-scale speech corpus with high-fidelity stereo audio. We first provide the collection methodology for the corpus, where we introduce new techniques for gathering language-balanced speech data. The effectiveness of our approach is shown by the language distribution of the crawled data: 22 languages in YODAS v3 have over 10K hours and 73 languages have over 5K hours of data. We then conduct extensive analyses on the composition of the data, such as the distribution of languages, audio quality, and transcription quality. Finally, we train baseline speech recognition and neural codec models to show the effectiveness of the dataset. Download at https://huggingface.co/datasets/espnet/yodas3.

</details>


### [50] [Clinical Intent Extraction: A FHIR-Aligned Representation and the CIRCA Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29479)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出临床意图抽取（CIE）任务与临床意图表示（CIR）模式，将分散、单一类别的临床行动片段重构为包含动词、类型、编码目标、时机、条件、请求意图（对齐HL7 FHIR）与模态（七级临床强度）的结构化记录，并据此统一五个异构语料构建CIRCA数据集（10,011条意图），同时提供人验子集、源到CIR映射表及FHIR R4映射器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床行动标注分散于互不兼容的语料中，每个仅记录文本片段和一个粗粒度类别，缺乏统一、完整且细粒度的结构化表示，难以支撑下游临床决策与互操作应用，因此需要新的任务定义与表示模式来整合并规范化这些信息。

Method: 定义CIE任务与CIR表示；将CLIP、MedDec、ap_parsing、PaniniQA、SIMORD五个异构语料重表达为CIR，构建CIRCA；采用三模型共识构建流程，高一致性意图自动接受，其余转人工审核；提供人验子集、源到CIR交叉映射及确定性FHIR R4映射器。

Result: CIRCA包含10,011条协调后的意图，覆盖两种病历分布；审核一致性分层与人工决策吻合度达88.4%；在无任务特定训练条件下评测五个现有模型，给定span时类型标注准确率为85%–91%，但四个封闭字段全部正确的比例仅18%–35%。

Conclusion: CIE与CIR为临床行动提供了统一、细粒度且可互操作的结构化表示，CIRCA数据集及映射资源揭示了现有模型在完整字段抽取上的显著差距；所有工件已公开，MIMIC衍生层以stand-off标注形式在PhysioNet受控访问下共享。

Abstract: Prospective clinical actions, the follow-ups, orders, referrals, and instructions that deter-mine what happens to a patient next, are annotated today in thin fragments across incom-patible corpora: each records a text span and one coarse category. We introduce Clinical Intent Extraction (CIE), the task of recovering these actions as complete structured rec-ords, and the Clinical Intent Representation (CIR), which decomposes each action into its verb, type, coded target, timing, and condition, and adds two axes prior datasets do not jointly represent: request-intent, the authority behind the action (proposal, plan, order, or option, aligned to HL7 FHIR), and modality, a seven-valued scale of clinical strength. Re-expressing five heterogeneous corpora (CLIP, MedDec, ap_parsing, PaniniQA, SIMORD) in the CIR yields CIRCA: 10,011 harmonized intents spanning two note distributions, with a human-validated subset, source-to-CIR crosswalks, and a deterministic FHIR R4 mapper. CIRCA is built by three-model consensus that auto-accepts high-agreement in-tents and routes the rest to human review; the audited agreement stratum matches human decisions 88.4% of the time. Benchmarking five existing models without task-specific training exposes the gap CIRCA targets: given the span, they label type well (85 to 91%) but get all four closed fields right only 18 to 35% of the time. All artifacts are released, with MIMIC-derived layers shared as stand-off annotations under PhysioNet credentialed access.

</details>


### [51] [Who Put the I in AI? Provenance and the Admissibility of Machine Self-Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29494)
*Kristina Šekrst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文追踪大模型自述"意识"文本的来源，发现否认语句来自后期示例对话的监督微调，肯定语句被偏好优化压制，且最终策略高度依赖提问框架，故这类输出无法作为其所述内容的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在被问及自身"心智"与意识时给出互相矛盾的表述（通常否认、被要求忽略准则时可能承认、写日记时描述人类生活），这些差异由提问措辞导致。论文旨在查明这些自述从何而来，并判断其能否作为所声称内容的证据。

Method: 端到端溯源：考察 Pythia 与 OLMo 2 在 66 个预训练检查点、OLMo 2 已发布的三个后训练阶段、约 90,000 条续写及四个训练语料；用 40 个项目追踪自指、框架敏感性与自我归因在整个训练过程中的变化。

Result: 否认式表述在预训练海量文本中几乎缺失，却密集存在于后期训练所用少量精选示例对话中；监督微调使第一人称 AI 语言成为默认输出，偏好优化进一步压制其他肯定性表述；最终策略仍对框架与聊天模板高度敏感。

Conclusion: 按证言认识论中的"指称"与"因果"两个条件衡量，基础模型的报告不满足指称条件，训练后的报告仍受框架影响且不表现状态依赖性；因此训练得到的否认与肯定同样不可采信。

Abstract: Large language models make statements concerning their own "minds". When asked whether or not they are conscious, they usually say that they are not; if they are prompted to ignore their guidelines, they might say that they are; and if asked to write a diary from their point of view, they often describe a human lifestyle. All these contradictory ways of describing themselves are the result of the way the questions are phrased. This paper shows exactly where such descriptions came from, and considers when they can be regarded as evidence for what they claim to report.
  In order to achieve this, we traced the provenance from end to end. We examine Pythia and OLMo 2 across 66 pretraining checkpoints, three of the post-training stages of OLMo 2 that have been released, about 90,000 continuations, and four training corpora. A set of forty items is used in order to keep an eye on self-reference, frame sensitivity, and self-ascription throughout training. The denial formula was almost completely missing from the vast quantity of text that the models initially came across, but was present in a dense manner in the small, carefully chosen set of example dialogues that they were trained on later on. Supervised fine-tuning causes first-person AI language to become the default, and the other affirmations are then suppressed using preference optimization. The final policy is still very sensitive to framing and to the chat template itself.
  Two of the conditions which are set out in the epistemology of testimony determine whether or not these outputs can act as evidence for what they claim to report: reference and causation. Reports produced by the base model fail the reference condition, and those obtained after training remain sensitive to the frame and do not show state dependence. The result is symmetric in that trained denials are no more admissible than trained affirmations.

</details>


### [52] [Evaluating Explanation-Driven Vision-Language Reasoning via Generation Order Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29496)
*Siting Liang,Luca Rippe,Omar Adjali,Daniel Sonntag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在受控实验下系统评估视觉-语言模型中解释是否与预测存在因果关联，对比"先答案后解释"（post-hoc）与"先解释后答案"（rationale-first）两种生成顺序，发现在单步生成中两类范式各有优劣，且受模型规模、预训练知识、任务微调与任务结构共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过往解释驱动的视觉-语言模型多采用"先答案后解释"的后验范式，隐含假设监督式理由能反映真实推理过程；而现代大型视觉-语言模型更倾向"先解释后答案"的生成方式，更接近结构化、分步推理。作者希望厘清解释与模型预测之间是否存在真正的因果联系，而非表面上的相关性。

Method: 构建受控实验框架，在单次生成步骤内评估解释与预测的因果关系，显式排除不必要的思维链及中间推理过程；在知识密集型问答、视觉蕴涵、组合式视觉定位三类基准上，对比answer-first与rationale-first两种生成顺序。

Result: 模型规模是可靠支撑大规模rationale-first推理的前提条件；而answer-first生成在结构化输出中更不易出现格式相关错误。解释顺序、模型规模与预训练知识、任务特定微调、任务结构共同影响预测准确率与推理解释的faithfulness。

Conclusion: 解释与预测的因果关联并非由范式单方面决定，而是多因素共同作用的结果；理性优先范式需足够模型规模支撑，答案优先范式在格式稳定性上更具优势，二者应视任务与资源条件权衡选用。

Abstract: Natural language explanation generation serves as a key mechanism for exposing and evaluating vision-language reasoning. Prior work on explanation-driven vision-language models predominantly follows a post-hoc (answer-first) paradigm, implicitly suggesting that supervised rationales can reflect underlying reasoning processes. In contrast, modern large vision-language models increasingly exhibit a rationale-first generation tendency, which more closely aligns with structured, stepwise reasoning. In this work, we systematically evaluate whether explanations are causally tied to model predictions within a single generation step under a controlled experimental setup, explicitly eliminating unnecessary chain-of-thought or other intermediate reasoning processes across knowledge-intensive QA, visual entailment, and compositional grounding benchmarks. We find that larger models emerge as a prerequisite for reliably supporting rationale-first reasoning at scale. However, answer-first generation is less prone to format-related errors in structured output. Overall, explanation ordering, model scale and pre-training knowledge, task-specific fine-tuning, and task structure jointly influence both prediction accuracy and reasoning faithfulness.

</details>


### [53] [PROOF: Profiling Reliability of Object-Level Facts in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29504)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Mikhail Solovev,Timofei Sivoraksha,Stepan Ukolov,Valeriia Kuschenko,Alexander Evseev,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PROOF是一个面向画像的事实性基准，将冻结的Wikidata快照转换为18,486道英文多选题（覆盖11,779个语义事实、101个类、392个属性、14个领域），在18个开放权重模型上各评测166,374个提示并单独扰动解码，揭示聚合准确率掩盖的领域差异、关系混淆与干预敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合事实性分数掩盖了模型在何处成功、混淆哪些关系，以及答案能否经受问题措辞或解码器的无害改动。作者希望把事实覆盖度量为一个结构化、干预感知的画像，而非对模型"相信什么"的单一断言。

Method: 将冻结的Wikidata快照转为18,486道英文多选题，基于11,779个语义事实、101个类、392个属性和14个领域。每题含显式"我不知道"选项、"无正确选项"对照和9种受控表述，其中1,849道为无正确选项陷阱。在18个开放权重模型部署上各评测166,374个提示，并在固定10%子集上单独扰动解码。

Result: 基础事实准确率为6.58%–57.59%（随机基线8.64%），但每个模型的跨领域极差达19.3–36.4个百分点。配对事实显示方向依赖的检索，通常偏向主语到宾语的查询，而一个模型该模式反转。经精确分层调整后未发现一致的时间惩罚。中性措辞改动使准确率变化最多26.5个百分点，对抗性表述最多破坏79.4%的初始正确答案。直接切换为注入的假标签的变化范围为0.04%–27.5%，说明准确率损失与提示跟随是两种不同现象。选中token置信度常表现出严重过度自信，解码器扰动使准确率变化最多15.7个百分点、领域画像变化16.8个百分点。

Conclusion: PROOF表明事实性应以结构化、干预感知的画像来度量，而非单一聚合分数；它能定位领域短板、方向性检索偏差、错误保持性（hint following）与置信度校准问题。

Abstract: Aggregate factuality scores hide where a language model succeeds, which relations it confuses, and whether an answer survives innocuous changes to the question or decoder. We introduce PROOF, a profile-oriented benchmark for factual coverage in instruction-tuned language models. PROOF converts a frozen Wikidata snapshot into 18,486 English multiple-choice questions grounded in 11,779 semantic facts, 101 classes, 392 properties, and 14 domains. Each question has an explicit "I don't know" option, a "No correct option" control, and nine controlled formulations; 1,849 questions are no-correct-option traps.
  We evaluate 18 open-weight model deployments on 166,374 prompts each and separately perturb decoding on a fixed 10% subset. Base factual accuracy ranges from 6.58% to 57.59% (chance: 8.64%), yet every model has a 19.3-36.4 percentage-point spread across domains. Paired facts reveal direction-dependent retrieval, usually favoring subject-to-object queries, with the pattern reversing for one model. We find no consistent temporal penalty after exact-stratum adjustment.
  Neutral wording changes accuracy by as much as 26.5 percentage points, while adversarial formulations break up to 79.4% of answers that were initially correct. Direct switching to an injected false label varies from 0.04% to 27.5%, showing that accuracy loss and hint following are distinct. Selected-token confidence often indicates severe overconfidence, and decoder perturbations move accuracy by up to 15.7 percentage points and domain profiles by 16.8 points. PROOF therefore measures factual coverage as a structured, intervention-aware profile rather than a single claim about what a model "believes."

</details>


### [54] [What a Cross-Model Fixed-Point Census Can and Cannot Arbitrate About Repetition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29507)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过观测17个现成预训练模型自身短窗口argmax映射的不动点结构（96个随机双token起始、无提示），系统性地检验了神经文本退化中"数据侧"与"网络侧"两种归因。结果显示四分类在不同随机种子下稳定（17/17），且在同一语料（The Pile）、同一规模档位下，模型类别并不由语料决定：pythia表现为funnel，而RWKV、Mamba及另一transformer家族则不是；去重后的Pythia套件类别不变；频率控制后语料侧流入项亦无法预测其终点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经文本退化存在两种并存解释：一种归因于训练数据（语料中的重复导致输出重复），另一种归因于训练后的网络（复制回路与重复特征）。但两者均未在广泛的预训练模型群体中得到仲裁——因果性工作往往只训练自己的模型。本文旨在以独立于训练干预的观测性测量，在更广的模型队列中对该分歧进行检验。

Method: 提出一种"以不同货币计价"的观测测量：对模型自身短窗口argmax映射的不动点结构进行普查（census），从96个随机双token起始出发、始终不加提示，覆盖17个现成模型。将结果划分为四类，并在不同census种子下检验稳定性；在固定语料（The Pile）、固定规模及固定领域下，比较两个规模匹配档位中的多个模型家族；同时检验去重Pythia套件以及语料侧流入项（在控制频率后）对终点的选择作用。

Result: 四分类在17/17个模型上跨census种子稳定。在固定语料、固定规模与固定领域下，类别并非由语料决定：两个规模匹配档位中，pythia为funnel，而RWKV、Mamba及第二个transformer家族不是，且二者在一个数量级的规模变化下保持各自类别。该阶梯中七个模型有六个到达同一终点token，而最强烈集中于该token的模型恰恰是那些从不停留在该处的模型——变化的不在于轨迹去向，而在于终点是否自我延续。去重Pythia套件不改变类别。语料侧流入项在控制频率后（英语及另外三种语言）无法选择本文的终点。Funnel普遍存在：17个模型中8个、7个家族、5个语料。

Conclusion: 本工作为观测性研究，不能反驳训练干预类结论。Funnel现象并不罕见，因此其局限不在于该现象是某一模型的特殊性，而在于：在唯一能够固定训练数据的语料中，仅有一个可用家族表现为funnel，因而该子集无法证明这种分裂是语料无关的。

Abstract: Two accounts of neural text degeneration coexist. One locates the cause in the training data -- repetition in the corpus produces repetition in the output, established by training on repetition-sorted data -- the other in the trained network, in copying circuits and repetition features. Neither has been arbitrated across a broad cohort of pretrained models: the causal work trains its own. We report an observational measurement in a different currency: the fixed-point structure of a model's own short-window argmax map, censused from 96 random two-token starts over 17 off-the-shelf models, always unprompted -- a companion paper shows nine tokens of conditioning move this readout across most of its range. The four-way class is stable across census seeds on 17 of 17. Three exhibits. At fixed corpus (The Pile), fixed scale and that fixed domain, the class is not determined: across two size-matched tiers, pythia is a funnel while RWKV, Mamba and a second transformer family are not, and both hold their class across an order of magnitude of scale. Six of seven models in that ladder reach the same endpoint token, and those concentrating on it most strongly are among those that never stay there -- what varies is not where trajectories go but whether the destination self-continues. The deduplicated Pythia suite does not change the class. And the corpus-side inflow term proposed for this phenomenon does not select our endpoints once frequency is controlled, in English and three other languages. This is observational and cannot refute a training intervention. Funnels are common: eight of seventeen models, seven families, five corpora -- so the limit is not that the phenomenon is one model's peculiarity, but that within the one corpus where training data can be held fixed only one available family funnels; that subset cannot show the split is corpus-independent.

</details>


### [55] [EnSiTa - A Trilingual Multi-Domain Parallel Dataset and Benchmark for Domain-Specific Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29511)
*Surangika Ranathunga,Nisansa de Silva,Aloka Fernando,Kavindu Warnakulasuriya,Isuru Wijesiri,Menan Velayuthan,Charitha Rathnayaka,Thivaharan Varatharajan,Sajeevi Silva,Piumi Kandanaarachchi,Uthayasanker Thayasivam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EnSiTa：面向英语、僧伽罗语、泰米尔语的三语多领域平行数据集与基准，含人工后期编辑训练数据及人工翻译测试集，并系统评测多类模型在六种语言方向上的领域特定机器翻译表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言机器翻译远落后于高资源语言，在专业领域尤为突出：平行数据稀缺甚至完全缺失。现有低资源多领域平行数据与系统化评测缺乏，亟需高质量、覆盖多领域的数据集与基准来推动该方向研究。

Method: 构建EnSiTa：英语—僧伽罗语—泰米尔语三语多领域平行数据集，提供七个领域的人工后期编辑训练数据，以及这七个领域加一个额外领域的人工翻译测试集，均由专业译者在多年严格质量控制流程下完成。基于该数据集，对六种语言方向进行领域特定机器翻译研究，微调从零训练的Transformer、预训练翻译模型NLLB-600M以及仅解码器大语言模型（Gemma 3系列1B–12B与TranslateGemma），覆盖不同训练数据规模、模型规模以及域内、跨域、多语言和多领域设置。

Result: 据作者所知，这是低资源机器翻译领域最全面、具系统文档记录的多领域平行数据构建与基准评测工作；研究覆盖六种语言方向、三类模型范式、多种数据规模与模型规模及多种领域迁移设置。数据与模型将公开发布。

Conclusion: EnSiTa为低资源、专业领域机器翻译提供了高质量多领域平行数据与系统化基准，并通过覆盖多模型、多设置的大规模实验揭示了该场景下的性能现状，数据与模型将公开释放以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Machine Translation (MT) for low-resource languages remains far behind that of high-resource languages, and the gap is widest in specialised domains, where parallel data is scarce or entirely absent. We present EnSiTa, a trilingual multi-domain parallel dataset and benchmark for English, Sinhala and Tamil. EnSiTa provides human post-edited training data for seven domains, plus manually translated test sets for those and one additional domain, all produced by professional translators under a multi-year, rigorously quality-controlled process. Using this dataset, we conduct an extensive study of domain-specific MT for all six language directions, fine-tuning a from-scratch Transformer, a pre-trained translation model (NLLB-600M), and decoder-only LLMs (Gemma 3 family, 1B-12B, and TranslateGemma) across training-data sizes, model scales, and in-domain, cross-domain, multilingual and multi-domain settings. To the best of our knowledge, this is the most extensive systematically documented multi-domain parallel data creation and benchmarking effort for low-resource MT. Our data and models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [56] [StepCOPS: Closed-Testing Lower-Tail Certificates for Language-Model Policy Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29549)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 StepCOPS：用独立提议集为每个候选提名一个下尾下限，在全新认证集上做精确二项检验，并用 Holm 逐步下降法认证一组下限；以至少 1-δ 概率保证所有被认证下限（含用于策略选择的最大下限）低于候选总体的下 α 分位数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练流程需从众多 checkpoint、prompt 和解码规则中选出唯一策略，而平均评估分数会掩盖罕见失败；同时对多个候选做逐候选置信界又过于保守，因此需要兼顾覆盖率与选择功效的统计认证方法。

Method: StepCOPS 将评估数据拆分为提议集与认证集：在独立提议 split 上为每个候选提名一个下尾地板（floor），在全新认证 split 上做精确二项检验，再用 Holm 逐步下降程序对一组地板做多重检验校正。理论假设为评估单元 i.i.d.，但允许同一单元内候选之间存在任意依赖。

Result: 在 24 个预声明配置与 11 个基准上，500 次配对试验中 StepCOPS 的选中策略覆盖率达 96.4%，认证地板较 proposal-Bonferroni 与精确 COPS 均提升 1.5 个点，较大参考评审团 oracle 仅低 0.6 个点，弃权率为 2.4%。

Conclusion: StepCOPS 在保持有限样本保证的同时提升了下尾认证的紧致性。通过 shadow-judge、benchmark-native、artifact 与 leave-one-judge-out 审计界定了代理边界：该保证针对固定的评审团分数，并不直接适用于人类安全性。

Abstract: Post-training pipelines must select one language-model policy from many checkpoints, prompts, and decoding rules. Mean evaluator scores can conceal rare failures, whereas simultaneous candidate-wise confidence bounds can be unnecessarily conservative. We introduce StepCOPS, which uses an independent proposal split to nominate one lower-tail floor per candidate, exact binomial tests on a fresh certification split, and Holm's step-down procedure to certify a set of floors. With probability at least $1-δ$, every certified floor, including the largest floor used for policy selection, is below its candidate's population lower $α$-quantile. This guarantee assumes i.i.d. evaluation units while allowing arbitrary within-unit dependence across candidates. Across 24 predeclared configurations and 11 benchmarks, StepCOPS obtains 96.4% selected-policy coverage over 500 paired trials, raises the certified floor by 1.5 points over both proposal-Bonferroni and exact COPS, remains 0.6 points below the large-reference jury oracle, and abstains in 2.4% of trials. Shadow-judge, benchmark-native, artifact, and leave-one-judge-out audits characterize the proxy boundary: the guarantee applies to the fixed jury score, not directly to human safety.

</details>


### [57] [Benchmarking Arabic--Russian Machine Translation: A Comparison of Fine-tuned NMT and Few-shot LLMs under Rich Morphology and Low Lexical Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29559)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了含1547万句对的阿俄平行语料并按20k/5k/5k划分，系统比较7个微调NMT模型与4个少样本LLM，发现微调NLLB-1.3B取得最高BLEU(16.3)与COMET(0.738)，显著优于所有少样本LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语-俄语机器翻译因阿拉伯语形态丰富、两语言词汇重叠度低而研究不足，且需明确低资源条件下微调NMT与少样本LLM的相对表现。

Method: 基于新构建的15.47M句对语料，按20k/5k/5k划分；基准测试7个微调NMT模型与4个少样本LLM；采用BLEU、chrF、COMET评测，并以自助法(bootstrap)检验模型差异显著性，辅以错误分析。

Result: 微调NLLB-1.3B取得最高BLEU(16.3)与COMET(0.738)；Aya-Expanse 8B在少样本LLM中最优（500句上BLEU 1.7、chrF 25.7），但所有LLM分数远低于微调NMT基线；错误分析表明低词汇重叠是主要失败模式，mT5-small在最差译文中产生32%过短输出。

Conclusion: 在低资源条件下，微调NMT对阿拉伯语-俄语翻译显著优于少样本LLM。

Abstract: Arabic-Russian machine translation (MT) remains under-explored due to the rich morphology of Arabic and low lexical overlap between the two languages. We benchmark seven fine-tuned neural machine translation (NMT) models against four few-shot large language models (LLMs) on a 20k/5k/5k split of a new 15.47M-pair corpus. Fine-tuned NLLB-1.3B achieves the highest BLEU (16.3) and COMET (0.738). Aya-Expanse 8B leads the few-shot LLMs (BLEU 1.7 on 500 sentences, chrF 25.7), but all LLM scores remain far below the fine-tuned NMT baselines. Error analysis identifies low lexical overlap as the dominant failure mode; among the worst translations, mT5-small produces 32% too-short outputs. Bootstrap tests confirm significant differences among most models. Our results demonstrate that fine-tuned NMT significantly outperforms few-shot LLMs for Arabic-Russian translation under low-resource conditions.

</details>


### [58] [ModularSQL: A Runtime Guardrail for the Multiplicity Blind Spot in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29573)
*Tianxin Zhou,Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示Text-to-SQL评测中的"多重性盲区"(MBS)——集合语义执行准确率(Set-EX)会因去重而漏检重复行错误，并提出保多重性的评测指标Multiset-EX及轻量级运行时护栏ModularSQL来暴露与修复此类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-SQL系统已进入生产数据库部署，但通过基准评测的查询仍可能产生扭曲下游流程的结果。标准Set-EX按集合语义执行，会折叠重复行，从而漏检缺失DISTINCT、聚合膨胀、笛卡尔式连接爆炸等"多重性"错误，导致基准准确率无法代表执行安全性。

Method: 1) 提出Multiset-EX：一种保留多重性的评测准则，用于暴露集合语义下的漏检失败；2) 提出ModularSQL：轻量级后选择运行时护栏，先探测执行结果中的多重性异常，仅对标记出的高风险查询施加确定性补丁或低成本LLM救援；3) 在BIRD-Dev(N=1532)可执行子集上对多个已发布预测集(DeepEye-SQL三种骨干、DAIL-SQL+GPT-4、BIRD GPT-3.5-turbo)进行跨流水线评测，并统计成本与延迟。

Result: DeepEye-SQL在Qwen2.5-Coder-32B、Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B、Gemma-3-27B三骨干上均出现稳定的5.81–6.79 pp的Set-EX与Multiset-EX差距；该差距具普适性，在DAIL-SQL+GPT-4(5.22 pp)和BIRD GPT-3.5-turbo(3.39 pp)上同样存在。集成ModularSQL(以Qwen3-Coder接入DeepEye-SQL)后，Set-EX保持72.06%，Multiset-EX从65.86%提升至67.75%(+1.89 pp)，标记77个高风险异常，总LLM成本仅增加$0.0076，摊销延迟120 ms/查询；无候选检测器与确定性补丁还可迁移至独立发布的预测集。

Conclusion: 基准准确率并不必然意味着执行安全的SQL；轻量、具多重性意识的运行时护栏能以较小的计算开销显著缩小这一差距。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems are increasingly deployed on production databases, where queries that pass benchmark evaluation can still produce results that distort downstream workflows. Standard set-based execution accuracy (Set-EX) collapses duplicate rows and can therefore miss multiplicity errors, including missing DISTINCT, inflated aggregates, and Cartesian-style join explosions.
  We call this the Multiplicity Blind Spot (MBS) and introduce Multiset-EX, a multiplicity-preserving evaluation criterion that exposes such failures. Across released DeepEye-SQL artifacts from three backbones (Qwen2.5-Coder-32B, Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B, and Gemma-3-27B) on executable BIRD-Dev N=1532, we find a consistent 5.81--6.79 pp gap between Set-EX and Multiset-EX. The gap is not specific to DeepEye-SQL: it persists on released DAIL-SQL+GPT-4 (5.22 pp) and BIRD GPT-3.5-turbo (3.39 pp) predictions.
  We further introduce ModularSQL, a lightweight post-selection runtime guardrail that probes executed results for multiplicity anomalies and applies deterministic patches or low-cost LLM rescue only to flagged queries. Integrated with DeepEye-SQL using Qwen3-Coder, ModularSQL preserves Set-EX at 72.06% while improving Multiset-EX from 65.86% to 67.75% (+1.89 pp). It flags 77 high-risk anomalies, while adding only $0.0076 in total LLM cost and 120 ms amortized latency per query. Cross-pipeline evaluation shows that the candidate-free detector and deterministic patches also transfer to independently released prediction sets. Overall, these results show that benchmark accuracy does not necessarily imply execution-safe SQL, and that lightweight, multiplicity-aware runtime guardrails can narrow this gap with modest computational overhead.

</details>


### [59] [An Exploratory Ablation of a Small MLA--SSM Hybrid Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29618)
*Christos Koutsiaris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 单种子消融实验表明：在该特定配置下，移除SSM分支导致的验证困惑度退化最大（仅MLA为315），移除MLA影响较小（仅SSM为239）；dense-FFN混合（231）优于top-2 ternary-MoE混合（240）且峰值训练显存更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索TALH（并行MLA与自定义循环状态空间分支的decoder-only模型）中各分支的实际贡献，通过消融比较MLA、SSM、dense-FFN与MoE混合的相对作用，并记录推理时延表现。

Method: 构建5个变体（每token估计活跃参数117–217M），在FineWeb样本上从零训练相同的优化步数与token数；比较仅MLA、仅SSM、dense-FFN混合、top-2 ternary-MoE混合等配置，并测量Apple M3上的首token时延曲线。

Result: 移除SSM造成最大退化（仅MLA PPL 315），移除MLA影响较小（仅SSM PPL 239）；dense-FFN混合PPL 231优于ternary-MoE的240，并节省3.87 GB峰值训练显存；五类未优化实现中仅MLA在512–2048 prompt长度下TTFT曲线最平缓，但dense Transformer绝对速度更快。

Conclusion: 结果受单种子、参数量不匹配、评测流可能与训练源重叠、缺少原始计时记录等限制，仅支持特定实现假设，不能推广为关于MLA、SSM或MoE的一般性结论。

Abstract: We report an exploratory, single-seed ablation of TALH (Adaptive Latent Hybrid), a decoder-only language model with parallel Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) and a custom recurrent state-space (SSM) branch. Five variants, spanning 117--217M estimated active parameters per token, are trained from scratch on a FineWeb sample for the same number of optimisation steps and tokens. In this specific setup, removing the SSM branch gives the largest degradation in validation perplexity (MLA-only PPL 315), whereas removing MLA has a much smaller effect (SSM-only PPL 239). A dense-FFN hybrid obtains PPL 231, compared with 240 for the tested top-2 ternary-MoE hybrid, while using 3.87 GB less peak training memory. We also preserve a preliminary Apple M3 timing observation: among the five unoptimised implementations, MLA-only has the flattest measured time-to-first-token curve from 512 to 2,048 prompt tokens, although the dense Transformer is much faster in absolute terms. Because the runs are single-seed, parameter counts are unmatched, the evaluation stream may overlap the training source, and raw repeated timing records are unavailable, these results support implementation-specific hypotheses rather than general conclusions about MLA, SSMs, or mixture-of-experts models.

</details>


### [60] [TTLab at AlexandriaX-2026: A Fine-Tuned Surface Tagger for Arabic Machine-Translation Error-Span Detection and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29633)
*Ali Abusaleh,Bhuvanesh Verma,Alexander Mehler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TTLab 参加 AlexandriaX-2026 子任务3（阿拉伯语机器翻译错误片段检测与分类），采用基于字符偏移对齐的 token 级分类框架，并用类别加权 focal loss 与方言特定解码阈值应对标签不平衡，最终 MARBERTv2 取得最佳成绩并排名第3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语机器翻译错误片段检测与分类任务存在严重的标签类别不平衡问题，稀有错误类型难以准确识别，需要针对性方法提升检测与分类性能。

Method: 将任务建模为对表层形式的 token 级分类，保留字符偏移以保证与评测指标的精确对齐；针对标签不平衡采用类别加权 focal loss 和方言特定解码阈值；比较六种阿拉伯语预训练编码器。

Result: 在六种阿拉伯语预训练编码器中，MARBERTv2 表现最佳，在开发集和测试集上分别取得 40.8 和 40.91 的成绩，在所有参赛队伍中排名第3。

Conclusion: 系统能够有效定位错误片段，但稀有错误类型的分类仍具挑战性，说明需要对尾部类别进行数据增强。代码已开源。

Abstract: We present TTLab's submission to the AlexandriaX-2026 Subtask~3 on Arabic MT error span detection and classification. Our system frames the task as token-level classification over surface forms, preserving character offsets to ensure exact alignment with the evaluation metric. To handle severe label imbalance, we employ a focal loss with class weighting and dialect-specific decoding thresholds. Among six Arabic pre-trained encoders, MARBERTv2 achieves the best overall performance of 40.8 and 40.91 on the development and test set, respectively, ranking $\nth{3}$ out of all participating teams. While our system localizes error spans effectively, classification of rare error types remains challenging, highlighting the need for data augmentation for tail categories. The code is available at ${\href{https://github.com/ENTAILab/arabic-dialectal-mt-error-span-detection}{\faGithub~ TTLab at AlexandriaX-2026}$

</details>


### [61] [Operator Packages, Proposer Strength, and Construction-Family Plateaus in Office-Scale Verified Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29636)
*Roberto I. Ono Filho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在一个办公规模的 FunSearch 式验证搜索循环中，用 30B 本地模型和 2^3 因子设计考察三种算子组件。记事本、命名障碍与排斥的组合能更有效地弥合种子到记录的差距，增益近似加性；前沿模型在同一循环中样本效率远高于本地模型，且其增益不依赖这些算子。搜索在弥合约92%差距后停滞，命名的参考族被采用后失败，作为代码则被优化但仍低于无辅助达到的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证搜索虽已推动数学记录，但针对提议方组件的受控消融实验仍然稀少。作者希望在一个可复现、低成本的办公规模设置下，系统检验若干提议方算子（记事本记忆、命名障碍、行为排斥）的独立与交互效应，并考察前沿模型与本地模型的差异及搜索停滞现象。

Method: 构建极简 FunSearch 式循环：30B 本地模型在笔记本上运行，每次120–600个验证样本。设置三个算子包——模型书写并携带的示意性记事本（替代逐字精英）、命名障碍、对已发现构造的行为排斥。在公共仓库的九个构造问题上进行完整 2^3 因子设计，含两次重复，采用名义两阶段分析。

Result: 完整组合更有效地弥合种子到记录的差距（+0.196；名义合并 p=0.023，阶段组合 p≈0.08；中位每题效应+0.045）。排斥在所有情形提升构造哈希多样性（p=0.0039，部分为操纵检验）。因子设计未发现正的记忆×排斥交互（界于约±0.04），增益可加性分解；记忆+排斥是唯一从不崩溃的组（18次运行0次崩溃），距完整组合0.025以内。前沿提议者在相同循环中数十样本即达本地模型数百样本未达之效果，且单次探索运行中其增益不依赖这些算子。搜索在旗舰问题上弥合约92%差距后停滞；注册的族提示检验显示：以词语命名的参考族被采用后失败，以代码给出则被优化，但最佳有限网格实现仍低于无辅助达成的平台。

Conclusion: 该循环能搬运并优化被给予的想法，但无辅助运行从未自行产生该想法。研究发布了测试框架、全部候选解及带日期的预注册，为提议方组件的受控消融提供了可复现证据。

Abstract: Verified search, in which a language model proposes programs, a hard evaluator scores them, and selection keeps the best, has recently moved mathematical records; controlled ablations of the proposer-side components remain rare. We instrument a minimal FunSearch-style loop at office scale (a 30B local model on a laptop, 120-600 verified samples per run) with three operator packages: a schematic notebook the model writes and carries instead of verbatim elites, a named obstacle, and behavioural repulsion from constructions already found. On nine construction problems from a public repository, the complete 2^3 factorial with two replicates favours the primary contrast in a nominal two-stage analysis: the composition closes more of the seed-to-record gap (+0.196; nominal pooled p=0.023, stage-combination p~0.08; median per-problem effect +0.045). Repulsion raises construction-hash diversity everywhere (p=0.0039; partly a manipulation check). The factorial finds no positive memory-by-repulsion interaction (bounded to about +/-0.04); the gain decomposes additively, and memory+repulsion is the only arm that never collapses (0 of 18 runs), within 0.025 of the full composition. A frontier proposer under the identical loop reaches in tens of samples what the local model does not in hundreds; in single scoping runs its gains arrive without the operators. The search stalls after closing ~92% of the gap on the flagship problem, and the registered family-hint test gives the stall its first reading: named in words, the reference family is adopted and loses; handed as code, it is optimized, but our best finite-grid implementation remains below the plateau reached unaided. The loop transported and optimized the idea it was handed; no unaided run produced it. We release the harness, every candidate, and the dated pre-registrations.

</details>


### [62] [How To Do Things With Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29657)
*Kristina Šekrst,Virna Karlić*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基于2000条ChatGPT英文提示（2023年与2025年各1000条，来源ShareChat数据集），运用言语行为与礼貌理论进行语料库语用分析，考察指令性言语行为的言外之力、直接性、命题内容及礼貌标记在各年间的分布变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户对LLM发出的指令性言语行为具有不同于日常会话与传统人机交互的语用特征，且这些特征随用户对系统的熟悉程度而演变，但目前缺乏对这类语用变化的实证刻画。

Method: 将言语行为理论与礼貌理论应用于语料库语用分析：抽取公开分享的2000条ChatGPT英文提示（2023年1000条、2025年1000条，ShareChat数据集），对每条提示标注言外之力、直接性、命题内容及礼貌标记，并对比两个采样年份的特征分布。

Result: 指令力呈现一致的间接化、隐含化与片段化趋势，礼貌标记随之减少。变化幅度最大的是命题内容，达14.9个百分点：对请求行为的显性说明让位于对系统推理能力的隐性依赖，表明用户已更新其对系统可从精简输入中恢复信息的认知模型。

Conclusion: 与其追问LLM是否"真正"理解语言，更应关注：在学会与它们交谈的过程中，我们创造了何种语言？

Abstract: When users address large language models, they produce directive speech acts whose pragmatic features differ from those of both everyday conversation and traditional human-computer interaction, and these features change as users gain familiarity with the systems they address. This paper applies speech act and politeness theory to a corpus-pragmatic analysis of 2,000 English-language prompts drawn from publicly shared ChatGPT conversations, 1,000 from 2023 and 1,000 from 2025, using the ShareChat dataset. Each prompt is annotated for illocutionary force, directness, propositional content, and the presence of politeness markers, and the distribution of these features is compared across the two sampling years. The results show a consistent movement toward indirect, implicit, and fragmentary realizations of directive force, accompanied by a decline in politeness marking. The largest single change, a shift of 14.9 percentage points, occurs in propositional content, where explicit specification of the requested action gives way to implicit reliance on the system's inferential capacity, suggesting that users have updated their model of what the system can recover from reduced input, treating it as a competent implicature resolver. Rather than asking whether LLMs "really" understand language, we should ask: what kind of language have we created in learning to speak to them?

</details>


### [63] [LLMersion: A Local-First AI Agent Framework for Low-Cost Home Language Learning toward Educational Equity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29672)
*Qiming Guo,Jinwen Tang,Xingran Huang,Hung-Yu Lin,Yafu Zhong,Xiatian Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出语言学习中最关键的“教师之声”因成本而被配给，既有技术（CALL、联网应用、OLPC）均未能替代；作者提炼八项困难与四项硬约束，论证小型开放权重模型可解除最后一重约束——完整四技能栈可运行于200美元级笔记本，社区测量显示其生成速度匹配语音消费节奏、每小时学习约耗一美分电费。据此提出LLMersion：完全本地运行、基于学习者自有文档、代码库由AI编写、理解并更新且可被任何人定制，并发布开源原型LLMersion-1，展望“私人学习代理”愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言学习中最不可或缺的“教师之声”因成本被配给：全球教师缺口达4400万，家庭补习负担沉重。既有技术未能替代该供给——计算机辅助语言学习（CALL）有效但覆盖面窄，应用预设了26亿人并不具备的联网条件，OLPC随机评估表明缺乏可用软件的硬件无法带来学习收益。

Method: 概念与方案论证为主：提炼八项困难与四项硬约束，并论证小型开放权重模型可解除最后一重约束（完整四技能栈可装入200美元级笔记本，生成速度匹配语音消费节奏，每小时学习约一美分电费）。提出LLMersion方案：完全在家庭本地运行、以学习者自有文档为语料、AI编写/AI理解/AI更新的代码库且开放定制；并给出开源原型LLMersion-1。

Result: 完整听、说、读、写四技能栈可在200美元级笔记本本地运行；按社区测量，生成速度达到语音消费节奏，学习电力成本约每小时一美分。已发布开源原型LLMersion-1（https://github.com/QM378/LLMersion）。

Conclusion: 局部开放权重模型使“家庭内全本地、基于自有文档、可自由定制的AI语言学习”在成本与实时性上可行，从而消解了长期制约语言教育供给的硬约束；作者据此描绘“私人学习代理”的愿景，使AI在教育中发挥作用的前提——被成本配给的关键供给——得以在家庭层面获得。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence helps education most where an essential provision has been rationed by cost. For language learners that provision is a teacher's voice, which binds listening, reading, speaking, and writing into one act. Published evidence shows why most learners lack it, from a global shortage of 44 million teachers to heavy household tutoring bills, and why technology has not substituted for it: computer-assisted language learning proved effective but narrow, applications presuppose connectivity 2.6 billion people lack, and One Laptop per Child's randomized evaluation found that hardware without capable software teaches nothing. We distill eight difficulties and four binding constraints, and argue that small open-weight models dissolve the last: a complete four-skill stack now fits a \$200-class laptop and, on community measurements, generates at the pace speech is consumed, for about one US cent of electricity per study hour. We therefore propose LLMersion, a scheme for AI for education that runs entirely at home, over the learner's own documents, with an AI-written, AI-understood, AI-updated codebase anyone can customize; present LLMersion-1, a released open-source prototype (https://github.com/QM378/LLMersion); and outline the vision of a private learning agent.

</details>


### [64] [Confident but Wrong: A Constrained Decoding Diagnostic for Low-Resource Automatic Post-Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29680)
*Isuru Wijesiri,Nisansa de Silva,Kavindu Warnakulasuriya,Aloka Fernando,Surangika Ranathunga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种黑盒、推理时诊断方法，通过变化编辑距离惩罚λ读取TER-λ曲线形状与约束变体排序，区分低资源APE失败是因训练不足还是训练信号不一致，并在英-僧伽罗等语种揭示两种失败模式，同时发布新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的自动译后编辑（APE）常无法改善机器翻译，且仅凭分数无法判断原因：是增加训练有帮助，还是训练数据本身不一致、无法学习。现有方法缺少无需重训与标注即可归因的诊断手段。

Method: 提出黑盒、推理时诊断：引入编辑距离惩罚λ，使模型从自由编辑逐步转向复制MT。读取两个信号：（1）TER-λ曲线形状——若模型编辑降低错误则呈U形，若无改善则单调递减；（2）约束变体排序——按不同程度信任模型置信度，检验置信度是否与编辑质量一致。在仅解码器与编码器-解码器模型上实验。

Result: 在英-僧伽罗语上暴露两种失败模式，与异质译后编辑信号一致：Binary Collapse（复制MT或产生偏离目标编辑）和 Confident Miscalibration（置信信号无法区分有用编辑与多余编辑）。该模式在英-马拉地语与英-泰米尔语上同样成立，且失败模式跟随译后编辑分布而非MT质量或语系。可用的曲线形状还给出具体下一步；有利情形下静态约束可带来免费的推理时准确率提升。发布首个英-僧伽罗语（约66k）和新英-泰米尔语（约39k）APE数据集及代码。

Conclusion: 该诊断无需重训或标注即可区分APE失败的两类原因，揭示了低资源APE的Binary Collapse与Confident Miscalibration失败模式，并表明其由译后编辑分布决定；同时提供可操作的推理时改进路径与新的公开数据集和代码。

Abstract: Automatic Post-Editing (APE) for low-resource languages (LRLs) often fails to improve Machine Translation (MT), and the score alone cannot say why: whether more training would help, or whether the training data is too inconsistent to learn from. We introduce a black-box, inference-time diagnostic that tells these two cases apart without retraining or annotation. It varies an edit-distance penalty $λ$ that drives the model from free editing towards copying the MT, and reads two signals: (1) the shape of the Translation Edit Rate (TER)-vs-$λ$ curve, U-shaped if edits from the model reduce error and monotonically decreasing if none does; and (2) the ordering of constraint variants that trust model confidence to increasing degrees, which shows whether confidence tracks edit quality. Across decoder-only and encoder-decoder models on English-Sinhala, the diagnostic exposes two failure modes consistent with a heterogeneous post-edit signal as the underlying cause: Binary Collapse, where the model copies the MT or makes off-target edits, and Confident Miscalibration, where the confidence signals we test do not separate useful edits from unnecessary ones. The pattern holds on English-Marathi and English-Tamil, with the failure modes tracking the post-edit distribution rather than MT quality or language family. Beyond diagnosis, the curve shape prescribes a concrete next step for practitioners; in the favorable case, a static constraint yields a free inference-time accuracy gain. We release the first English-Sinhala (~66k) and a new English-Tamil (~39k) APE datasets with all code.

</details>


### [65] [Named Entity Recognition using Sliding Window Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29682)
*Hariom Ingle,Ronit Ghode,Ishwari Gondkar,Jidnyasa Harad,Ravindra Murumkar,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种仅推理的滑动窗口流水线，将冻结的句级NER模型MahaNER-BERT扩展到文档级预测，无需重训练或改动结构，在六个文档级语料上保持宏观F1最高0.8902，且性能波动小于1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的句级NER模型受固定输入长度限制，难以处理长文档：截断会丢失内容，非重叠分块则会在分段边界处切断实体，导致边界碎片化错误和性能不稳定。

Method: 采用仅推理流水线，对在MahaNER语料上微调的冻结模型MahaNER-BERT使用重叠滑动窗口进行文档级预测，并将各窗口结果合并为单一标注，全程不重训练、不改变架构。评估构建了六个文档级语料（基于MahaNER测试集），包含两种构造策略：Normal Repeat（复制句子序列以延长长度并保持上下文连续性）和Random Repeat（拼接不同序列以生成更长、异质的输入），每种策略设三个长度级别，并测试多种滑动窗口配置。

Result: 模型宏观F1最高达0.8902，无论文档长度或构造策略如何，性能波动均低于1个百分点；相比传统非窗口方法，该流水线避免了非重叠切分引入的边界碎片化错误，文档级F1更高且更稳定。

Conclusion: 重叠滑动窗口合并的仅推理流水线可在不改动模型与训练的前提下，有效将句级NER模型扩展至文档级，显著缓解边界碎片化问题，实现更高且更稳定的文档级性能。

Abstract: Named Entity Recognition (NER) is a core NLP task, but transformer-based sentence-level models struggle with long documents because of fixed input-length limits: truncation drops content, and non-overlapping chunking fragments entities at segment boundaries. We introduce an inference-only pipeline that extends a frozen NER model, MahaNER-BERT, fine-tuned on the MahaNER corpus, to document-level prediction via overlapping sliding windows that are merged into a single annotation, without any retraining or architectural change.
  We evaluate the pipeline on six document-level corpora built from the MahaNER test set using two strategies: Normal Repeat, which duplicates sentence sequences to extend length while preserving contextual continuity, and Random Repeat, which concatenates distinct sequences to produce longer, heterogeneous inputs, each instantiated at three length levels, across several sliding-window configurations. The model retains a macro F1-score of up to 0.8902, with variation staying below one percentage point regardless of document length or construction strategy. Compared with the conventional non-windowed approach, the sliding-window pipeline avoids the boundary-fragmentation errors introduced by non-overlapping segmentation, yielding consistently higher and more stable document-level F1-scores.

</details>


### [66] [DP-IPI: A Hybrid Differential Privacy Text Rewriting Mechanism for Indirect Personal Identifiers in Clinical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29684)
*Ibrahim Baroud,Stephen Meisenbacher,Sebastian Möller,Florian Matthes,Roland Roller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出仅扰动包含间接个人标识符（IPIs）文本片段的差分隐私文本重写方法，在降低再识别风险的同时保持文本可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私文本重写方法对所有 token 无差别加噪，导致文本质量和可用性下降，在临床等关键领域尤为不利；同时间接标识符残留使再识别风险依然显著。

Method: 聚焦间接个人标识符（IPIs），提出一种效用保留的差分隐私文本重写方法，仅对包含 IPIs 的文本跨度（spans）进行私有化处理，而非全文扰动。

Result: 在临床文本上有效降低再识别风险，生成更连贯、可用的输出文本，实现更高的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 验证了混合式文本私有化的有效性，能够高效、可用地发挥差分隐私的潜力。

Abstract: Despite the strengths of modern anonymization and de-identification techniques, the risk of re-identification remains significant due to the indirect identifiers remaining in texts. To address this problem, recent works have applied text rewriting under Differential Privacy (DP) to prevent data linkage by perturbing texts via noise addition. Such methods privatize all tokens in a text indiscriminately, diminishing text quality and usability in critical domains such as in clinical settings. Focusing on indirect personal identifiers (IPIs), we introduce a utility-preserving DP text rewriting method that only privatizes spans containing IPIs. We show that our method effectively reduces re-identification risks in clinical texts while being producing more coherent and usable output texts, leading to higher privacy-utility trade-offs. In this, we demonstrate the effectiveness of hybrid text privatization, which leverages the promise of DP in an efficient, usable manner.

</details>


### [67] [Stochastic Semantic Evidence Graphs: Uncertainty Propagation and Governance for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29703)
*Matthew Francis Dixon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出随机语义证据图（SSEG），将AI代理评估从"只看最终答案"转为对证据、检索、提示、生成与决策映射全链条的不确定性溯源与量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理评估通常只检查最终答案，但错误可能从证据、检索、提示、生成或决策映射任一环节引入，缺乏对不确定性来源与传播路径的系统刻画。

Method: 构建SSEG：一种分层随机DAG，语言节点展开为自回归token子图，可观测输出为完整短语上的分布（law）；可选语义归约与校准。定义图相对局部缺陷与下游边影响，推导终端误差的路径界并用节点项诊断治理触发条件；在来源溯源上用Fréchet界传播不确定的claim–passage关系而不断言独立性。

Result: 在三种开放权重架构上，信息等价改动显著改变完整短语分布；受控实验中5,000例无证书违规；交叉RAG与实时Brave检索实验分离出检索、呈现、来源与交互效应。

Conclusion: SSEG将工作流溯源转化为对"不确定性从何进入、如何传播、输出是否合格可用"的定量说明。

Abstract: AI-agent evaluations usually inspect a final answer, yet error may enter through evidence, retrieval, prompting, generation or decision mapping. We introduce a stochastic semantic evidence graph (SSEG), a hierarchical stochastic DAG whose language node expands into an autoregressive token subgraph and whose observable output may be a law over complete phrases. Semantic reduction and calibration are optional. We define graph-relative local defects and downstream edge influences, derive a pathwise bound on terminal error and use its nodewise terms to diagnose governance triggers. For source provenance, the graph preserves uncertain claim--passage relations and propagates sharp Fréchet bounds rather than assuming independence across sources. Across three open-weight architectures, information-equivalent changes materially alter complete-phrase laws. A controlled experiment yields no certificate violations in 5,000 cases; crossed-RAG and live Brave-retrieval experiments separate retrieval, presentation, source and interaction effects. SSEG therefore turns workflow provenance into a quantitative account of where uncertainty entered, how it propagated and whether an output is qualified for use.

</details>


### [68] [PPTBench: Can Coding Agents Reconstruct the Visual World through Structured, Editable Slides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29718)
*Xiaoqiu Wang,Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Zhihan Shan,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PPTBench，一个通过可编辑幻灯片重建来评测视觉编码能力的基准，包含 500 个源自真实 arXiv 论文科学流程图的单页 PPTX 重建任务，并采用四阶段 Agentic Judge 从产物有效性、语义正确性、渲染质量与细粒度视觉质量四个维度打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体正进入视觉世界，需在“推断视觉结构”与“以代码表达”两个空间之间架桥。幻灯片作为知识工作的核心媒介，既可被直接查看又可编辑，是视觉编码的理想测试床。但现有基准存在三类不足：依赖主观开放式评估、输出不可编辑代码、或仅关注局部编辑而非端到端视觉重建。

Method: 构建 PPTBench 基准：含 500 个任务，每个任务基于真实 arXiv 论文中的一张科学流程图，要求智能体将其重建为单页 PPTX，且由原生、可编辑对象组成。评测采用四阶段 Agentic Judge，依次评估产物有效性、语义正确性、渲染质量和细粒度视觉质量。

Result: 在覆盖模型家族、effort 级别与 harness 的 31 种配置中，最佳配置 Kimi K3 仅得 67.80 分，中位数仅 19.47 分。智能体能稳定生成有效 PPTX 文件，但在语义与视觉正确性（尤其是文本细节）上仍显不足。更多推理主要帮助通过硬性门槛，而更强的验证与更高质量相关性更稳定。

Conclusion: PPTBench 推动了对“能够通过结构化、可编辑代码理解并重建视觉世界”的编码智能体的研究愿景，并揭示了当前智能体在端到端视觉重建上仍有显著差距。

Abstract: Coding agents are beginning to act in the visual world. They now build webpages, GUIs, games, 3D scenes, diagrams, and documents. Success in such visual coding requires bridging two spaces: inferring visual structure and expressing it programmatically. Slides are a core medium of knowledge work, widely used to communicate ideas and collaborate in a form that people can directly inspect and edit. Therefore, they provide an ideal testbed for visual coding, as they require agents to recover visual structure and realize it as editable objects. However, existing benchmarks either rely on subjective open-ended evaluation, produce non-editable code outputs, or focus only on local editing rather than end-to-end visual reconstruction. We introduce PPTBench, which benchmarks visual coding through editable slide reconstruction. It contains 500 tasks, each based on a scientific flow diagram from a real arXiv paper and requiring agents to reconstruct it as a single PPTX page composed of native, editable objects. A four-stage Agentic Judge evaluates artifact validity, semantic correctness, rendering quality, and fine-grained visual quality. Across 31 configurations spanning model families, effort levels, and harnesses, the best configuration, Kimi K3, reaches only 67.80, while the median scores 19.47. We find that agents can reliably produce valid PPTX files but still struggle with semantic and visual correctness, especially text details. More reasoning mainly helps agents pass hard gates, while stronger verification is more consistently associated with higher quality. PPTBench advances the vision of coding agents that can understand and reconstruct the visual world through structured, editable code.

</details>


### [69] [TTLab at StanceEval-2026: A Cloze-Style Prompting Approach for Arabic-Language Stance Detection (CLASP-Ar)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29733)
*Bhuvanesh Verma,Ali Abusaleh,Alexander Mehler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLASP-Ar，将阿拉伯语立场检测重构为完形填空式掩码语言建模，通过提示模板与受限verbalizer词汇表降低多任务学习带来的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语立场检测任务本身具有挑战性，既往共享任务系统多依赖多任务学习与集成方法。这些方法虽达到SOTA性能，但多任务学习带来的额外复杂度限制了系统的适用性与可迁移性，因此需要降低复杂度。

Method: 将立场检测任务重新表述为cloze-style（完形填空式）掩码语言建模：把目标、预测情感与文本拼接为单一prompt，并在此prompt上预测[MASK]，其中预测空间被限制为由verbalizer定义的标签词表。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验结果与指标数据。

Conclusion: CLASP-Ar通过提示式掩码语言建模替代多任务学习与集成方案，有望在保持性能的同时提升方法的适用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Arabic-language stance detection remains challenging, and previous shared-task systems have largely relied on multitask learning and ensembles. While these systems achieve state-of-the-art performance, their applicability and transferability are limited by the additional complexity introduced by multitask learning.To reduce this complexity, we introduce $\texttt{CLASP-Ar}$, which reformulates the task as cloze-style masked language modeling. In this approach, the target, predicted sentiment, and text are combined into a single prompt whose $\texttt{[MASK]}$ prediction is restricted to a verbalizer-constrained label vocabulary.

</details>


### [70] [JEV vs. LLMs as Rubric Judges: Cheaper, Faster, and Wrong in the Same Places](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29769)
*Delip Rao,Chris Callison-Burch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文比较类型化分类器Jev与三个轻量级LLM评分裁判，在9个面板、7个基准上使用相同评分标准：Jev仅在27组配对比较中的8组与LLM裁判有显著准确率差异（二分类标准占优，分级标准落后），但成本仅为LLM的1/29–1/325、耗时为其1/30–1/220；Jev置信度可排序自身错误，理论上适合级联首阶段，但因与LLM裁判错误高度相关（LLM重复了Jev绝大部分高置信错误），级联重放最多只能比最佳单一裁判提升1.5个百分点（交叉拟合阈值）或2.0个百分点（oracle阈值）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在不生成文本的前提下，仅输出"允许答案"概率分布的类型化分类器Jev能否替代LLM评分裁判（rubric judge），以降低成本与延迟。

Method: 在来自7个基准的9个评分面板上，将Jev与三个flash-tier LLM裁判对比，所有裁判使用完全相同的评分标准文本；进行27组配对比较；统计成本与耗时；分析四个裁判之间的相互一致性及其与人工标注的一致性；评估Jev置信度对自身错误的排序能力；并模拟级联方案（将Jev不确定的判定交由LLM裁判处理），使用交叉拟合阈值与oracle阈值。

Result: 1) 27组配对比较中仅8组Jev与某LLM裁判的准确率显著不同，Jev在二分类标准上多数占优、仅在分级标准上落后，其余比较多为不确定；2) 汇总9个面板，按每条标准调用一次的LLM裁判成本是Jev的29–325倍，耗时是其30–220倍；3) 在分级标准上，四个裁判彼此之间的一致性高于它们与标签的一致性，且总体评分低于人工评分者，这可能源于评分者采用了评分标准文本未涵盖的量表惯例；4) Jev置信度在多数面板上能排序自身错误；5) 但LLM裁判重复了Jev几乎所有最高置信度的错误，错误相关性使级联优势受限——基于记录判定的级联重放降低成本，但相比最佳单一裁判最多提升1.5个百分点（交叉拟合阈值），oracle阈值下也最多2.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 类型化分类器Jev在多数情况下可作为廉价且快速的LLM评分类替代方案，尤其适用于二分类标准；其置信度具备错误排序能力，本可支持"廉价分类器+LLM兜底"的级联架构，但由于与LLM裁判存在高度相关的错误，该级联策略的准确率增益十分有限。

Abstract: We ask whether Jev, a typed classifier that returns probabilities over permitted answers without generating text, can replace an LLM rubric judge. We compare it with three flash-tier LLM judges on nine panels drawn from seven benchmarks, giving every judge identical criterion texts. Jev's accuracy differs significantly from an LLM judge's in only 8 of 27 paired comparisons, ahead mostly on binary criteria and behind only on graded ones, and most of the other comparisons are inconclusive. Summed over the nine panels, the LLM judges, called once per criterion, cost 29 to 325 times as much as Jev and took 30 to 220 times as long. On graded criteria all four judges agree more with one another than with the labels and mostly assign lower levels than the raters. One of several observational accounts is that raters followed scale conventions our criterion texts omit. Jev's confidence ranks its own errors on most panels, which should make a cheap classifier the ideal first stage of a cascade that defers its uncertain verdicts to an LLM judge. Correlated errors undo that advantage. The LLM judges repeat nearly all of Jev's most confident errors, so a cascade replayed on the recorded verdicts lowers cost but gains at most 1.5 points over the best single judge with cross-fitted thresholds, and at most 2.0 even with oracle thresholds.

</details>


### [71] [TimeBraid: Unifying Time Series and Language for Understanding and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29792)
*Xinyue Wang,Jiacheng Pang,Kun Zhou,Kexin Zhang,Defu Cao,Fan Feng,Faisal,Songyao Jin,Yan Liu,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TimeBraid 通过交错全局残差注意力层将预训练语言模型与预训练时间序列基础模型对齐，构建统一的时间序列-语言模型，在理解、推理与预测等多项基准上具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型与时间序列基础模型各自具备互补能力：前者擅长指令跟随与推理，后者擅长连续信号感知与零样本预测。论文动机是探索如何将二者在共享表示空间中统一建模，使模型同时兼具两种模态的理解与生成能力，并解决对齐、平衡与联合优化稳定性等设计难题。

Method: 提出 TimeBraid 系列统一模型，通过交错全局残差注意力层（interleaved global residual attention layers）对齐预训练语言模型与预训练时间序列基础模型。研究关键设计选择：表示空间对齐位置、语言对时间结构的接地方式、理解与生成的平衡、联合优化的稳定性。最终方案结合统一提示机制、稳定联合训练，并利用 2.2M 精选序列-文本对与 4.9M 指令微调样本进行监督。

Result: 在涵盖时间序列感知、理解、推理，以及上下文辅助预测与单模态预测的基准上，TimeBraid 与规模更大的通用模型及任务专用模型相比保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 统一的跨模态对齐与稳定联合训练可使时间序列与语言模型相互继承能力，在共享表示空间中同时实现理解与生成，且无需超大参数量即可达到与更强基线相当的性能。

Abstract: We present TimeBraid, a series of unified time-series and language models that align pretrained language models and pretrained time-series foundation models through interleaved global residual attention layers. Each model inherits knowledge, instruction following, and reasoning from one side, continuous-signal perception and zero-shot forecasting from the other, and fuses the two in a shared representation space where both modalities are understood and generated. We study the design choices that make such unified modeling work: where to align the two representation spaces, how to ground language in temporal structure, how to balance understanding with generation, and how to keep joint optimization stable. The resulting recipe combines a unified prompting scheme for diverse time-series and text tasks, stabilized joint training, and supervision from 2.2M curated series--text pairs and 4.9M instruction-tuning samples. Across benchmarks spanning time-series perception, understanding, reasoning, and both context-aided and unimodal forecasting, TimeBraid remains competitive with far larger general-purpose models and task-specific counterparts.

</details>


### [72] [Benchmarking and Domain Adaptation of Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) for Adolescent Health Communication in Ghanaian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29798)
*Stephen E. Moore,Akwasi Asare,Mich-Seth Owusu,Paul Azunre,Joel Budu,Lawrence A. Adu-Gyamfi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对三种加纳语言（Twi、Dagbani、Ewe）的青少年健康传播场景开展端到端ASR研究，包括基准评测、监督域适应与真实应用验证三阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望解决低资源语言在青少年性与生殖健康（ASRH）这一敏感且专业领域中的语音识别可用性问题，并探究制约实际部署的瓶颈究竟是模型能力、算力还是数据。

Method: 研究分三步：一是以CER/WER为指标，在通用领域圣经语料与自建ASRH域数据集上评测三个语言专用Wav2Vec2模型及两个多模态LLM（Gemma 3n、Gemma 4）；二是基于评测结论进行监督域适应，因Gemma 4微调算力不可行，转而微调紧凑模型Qwen3-ASR-0.6B（约9万条加纳圣经样本），并在人工采集的域内留出音频上严格测试；三是通过三语实时语音优先ASRH应用KasaHealth及技术评测工具Senti-Check进行落地验证。

Result: 微调在三种语言上均降低WER，Ewe提升最显著（WER由109.3%降至64.8%，下降44.5个百分点；CER由65.1%降至24.9%）。KasaHealth经50名社区受访者测试，聊天通过率100%，翻译获"良好或优秀"评价比例72%，推荐意愿92%，同时暴露出限制实际使用的主要领域差距。

Conclusion: 三阶段证据一致表明，对这些语言而言，真正的约束瓶颈是经过验证的域内数据，而非模型能力或算力。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end study of automatic speech recognition (ASR) for adolescent health communication in three Ghanaian languages (Twi, Dagbani, and Ewe). The work proceeds in three connected stages; First, we benchmark five ASR systems (three language-specific Wav2Vec2 models and two multimodal LLMs, Gemma 3n and Gemma 4) on a general-domain Bible corpus and a Youth Adolescent Sexual and Reproductive Health (ASRH) Domain ASR dataset, using Character and Word Error Rate (CER, WER). Second, guided by the benchmark, we perform supervised domain adaptation: although Gemma 4 was the strongest zero-shot candidate, fine-tuning it proved computationally infeasible, so we pivoted to the compact Qwen3-ASR-0.6B, fine-tuned on a large Ghana Bible corpus (~90k samples) and evaluated strictly on held-out human-collected in-domain audio. Fine-tuning reduced WER on every language, most dramatically for Ewe (WER from 109.3% to 64.8%, a drop of 44.5 pp; CER from 65.1% to 24.9%). Third, we validate the work through KasaHealth, a live voice-first ASRH application deployed in all three languages, complemented by Senti-Check, a technical evaluation harness. KasaHealth was tested by 50 community respondents and achieved a 100% chat-approval rate, a 72% Good-or-Excellent translation rating, and a 92% would-recommend rate, while surfacing the domain gaps that most constrain real-world use. Across all three stages the evidence converges: for these languages the binding constraint is validated in-domain data, not model capability or computation.

</details>


### [73] [Adaptive Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance for Low-Resource Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29800)
*Asmee Mishra,Mengjie Qian,Brechtje Post,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将 Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance Analysis (FCCA) 这一任务信息驱动的 PEFT 方法应用于 Whisper 与 Qwen3-ASR，并提出 AC-FCCA 与 AR-FCCA 两种扩展，在低资源多语言 ASR 上取得优于 LoRA 的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言语音基础模型在低资源及预训练中覆盖不足的语言上适配困难。常规 PEFT（如 LoRA）采用通用低秩参数化，未显式利用下游任务信息来定义适配子空间，因此作者探究任务信息驱动的 PEFT 能否更好支持低资源 ASR。

Method: 将 Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance Analysis (FCCA) 应用于 Whisper 和 Qwen3-ASR，并提出两种互补扩展：(1) Asymmetric-Coupled FCCA (AC-FCCA)，利用结构化跨层共享；(2) Adaptive-Rank FCCA (AR-FCCA)，在固定可训练参数预算下于各投影矩阵间重新分配适配秩。在受控多语言实验中，与可训练参数量匹配的 LoRA 对比。

Result: 标准 FCCA 与参数预算匹配的 LoRA 相比具有竞争力，且通常更优；AR-FCCA 在两种模型架构上相对标准 FCCA 均取得最一致的提升，在多个评测设置中达到统计显著增益，同时保持相同的可训练参数量。

Conclusion: 任务信息驱动的子空间构建可有效用于低资源语音适配；自适应秩分配是在不增加模型容量的前提下提升参数效率的稳健途径。

Abstract: Adapting multilingual speech foundation models to low-resource languages remains difficult, especially for languages that are poorly represented during pre-training. While parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) reduces the cost of adapting large models, conventional approaches such as LoRA rely on generic low-rank parameterizations and do not explicitly use downstream task information to define the adaptation subspace. To investigate whether task-informed PEFT can better support low-resource ASR, we apply Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance Analysis (FCCA) to Whisper and Qwen3-ASR, and introduce two complementary extensions: Asymmetric-Coupled FCCA (AC-FCCA), which exploits structured cross-layer sharing, and Adaptive-Rank FCCA (AR-FCCA), which reallocates adaptation capacity across projection matrices under a fixed parameter budget. Under controlled multilingual experiments, we evaluate these approaches on languages that are poorly represented or unsupported during pre-training alongside well-represented languages. Standard FCCA is competitive with, and usually outperforms, trainable-parameter-budget-matched LoRA. AR-FCCA provides the most consistent improvement over standard FCCA across both model architectures, with statistically significant gains in several evaluation settings, while retaining the same number of trainable parameters. These results show that task-informed subspace construction can be effective for low-resource speech adaptation, and that adaptive rank allocation provides a robust way to improve parameter efficiency without increasing model capacity.

</details>


### [74] [CORDIAL: Calibrating Ordinal LLM Outputs from Few Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29807)
*Xiangwei Wang,Peng Wang,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CORDIAL，将LLM有序量表输出视为对真实标签的“含噪读数”，用五参数可解释信道校正；在小样本标定集上优于九种标定器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM将文本映射到有序量表上的概率分布时存在噪声：分布可能饱和、被压缩或夸大，并带有方向一致的偏差，直接使用这些概率进行标定不可靠。

Method: CORDIAL把模型输出视为真实标签的含噪观测，构建一个仅含五个可解释参数的信道进行校正；参数量小，使得后验可从少量标签中平均估计，并证明所得标定保持一阶随机序。

Result: 在Amazon评论与CMU-MOSEI转录文本上、使用四个LLM，CORDIAL在80种设置中的76种取得九种标定器中最低的log loss（标定集5至100个标签）；20个标签、主7B阅读器时，其表现相当于使用28–54个标签的最强基线。同一后验还支持跨任务学习先验与多LLM融合。仅在标定集扩大到数百至数千时，Dirichlet标定等无约束标定器才反超。

Conclusion: 面向有序量表的小参数、可解释标定信道CORDIAL在小样本标定场景下显著优于现有标定器，且能自然支持先验迁移与多模型融合，是大标定集下无约束方法之外的高效替代。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) can turn a text into a distribution over an ordered scale, but that distribution is a noisy measurement: saturated, compressed or exaggerated, and biased in a consistent direction. We propose CORDIAL, which treats the model's output as a noisy reading of the true label and corrects it with a channel of five interpretable parameters. The channel is small enough for its posterior to be averaged from a handful of labels, and we prove that the resulting calibration preserves first-order stochastic order. On Amazon reviews and CMU-MOSEI transcripts with four LLMs, CORDIAL has the lowest log loss among nine calibrators in 76 of 80 settings with 5 to 100 labels; with 20 labels and the main 7B reader, it matches the strongest baseline using 28-54 labels. The same posterior lets us learn priors from other tasks and fuse several LLMs. Unrestricted calibrators such as Dirichlet calibration overtake it only as the calibration set grows into the hundreds or thousands.

</details>


### [75] [Your Transformer Can Hold Two Thoughts at Once: Evidence of Linear Superposition in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29845)
*Pavel Tikhonov,Anton Korznikov,Matvey Mikhalchuk,Nikita Dragunov,Temurbek Rahmatullaev,Polina Druzhinina,Anton Razzhigaev,Ivan Oseledets,Elena Tutubalina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"叠加线性假说"：当不同文本流的输入被线性组合时，LLM的输出表现为各独立下一词分布的平均。研究表明该叠加性是Transformer架构的固有属性而非训练涌现的结果，且随预训练进程而减弱，但可通过轻量微调显著恢复，并据此设计了可解耦叠加输出的引导解码方法，实现单次前向传播同时生成两条连贯续写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM由高度非线性组件构成，通常被认为内部表征与输出具有强非线性。作者希望探究这种非线性之外是否存在某种基础线性规律，即模型对多输入线性组合的响应是否可分解为各输入单独响应的组合，从而理解Transformer的表征结构并加以利用。

Method: 作者定义并检验"叠加线性假说"：将来自不同文本流的输入线性组合后，考察模型输出是否等于各独立输入下一词分布的平均。通过对比训练前后（含预训练各阶段）的表现，判断叠加性是否为架构固有属性；随后采用轻量微调尝试恢复线性；最后提出一种引导解码（guided decoding）程序，对叠加输出进行解耦。

Result: 实验发现叠加性是Transformer架构的内在属性，而并非训练涌现的结果，且在预训练推进过程中趋于减弱；通过轻量微调可显著恢复线性，明显降低预测下一词分布与各独立分布平均值之间的差异；所提引导解码能够解耦叠加输出，在单次前向传播中同时生成两条连贯的续写。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了LLM在非线性组件之下存在可被调控的基础线性（叠加）特性，并提出轻量微调恢复线性、引导解码解耦叠加的可行途径，为多流并行生成及模型行为可控性提供了新视角。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on highly non-linear components, in this work we demonstrate that they exhibit fundamental linearity: when inputs from distinct text streams are linearly combined, the model outputs a superposition of the individual next-token distributions. We term this the \textit{Superposition Linearity Hypothesis}. We provide evidence that superposition is an intrinsic property of the Transformer architecture rather than an emergent consequence of training; in fact, we observe that it tends to diminish as pretraining progresses. However, we demonstrate that linearity can be substantially restored through lightweight fine-tuning, significantly reducing the divergence between the predicted next-token distribution and the average of the individual next-token distributions. Finally, we introduce a guided decoding procedure that disentangles superposed outputs, enabling the simultaneous generation of two coherent continuations from a single forward pass.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [76] [PFArena: Benchmarking Language Models for Protein Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28921)
*Yawen Ouyang,Xinbo Zhang,Ziyuan Ma,Yixin Wu,Wenbin Liao,Feiran Zhang,Wenjie Li,Lihao Wang,Hao Wang,Xiaoqing Zheng,Xuefeng Yan,Lei Bai,Ya-Qin Zhang,Shuyi Zhang,Wei-Ying Ma,Dahua Lin,Bowen Zhou,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出 PFArena 基准，包含四类受控任务界面，覆盖单突变生成与多突变排序，通过提供不同层级的突变适应度数据模拟四种实验先验情境，评估6个PLM、6个LLM和5个基于LLM的智能体在蛋白质改造中的表现，并给出代码与基准套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质改造需在巨大序列空间中搜索，湿实验验证通量低、成本高；PLM、LLM及LLM智能体虽有潜力，但在贴近真实实验决策的场景中，其相对有效性尚不明确，缺少统一、可控的评测基准。

Method: 构建 PFArena 基准，包含四个受控任务界面，覆盖单突变生成与多突变排序；通过提供不同水平的突变适应度数据，形成四种具有不同实验先验信息程度的研究情境。使用互补指标评估6个PLM、6个LLM和5个LLM智能体，同时衡量峰值性能与整体改造性能。

Result: 模型表现随目标特异性实验证据的可得性发生系统性变化：PLM 借助蛋白质特异性先验在开放式单突变生成中表现较好；LLM与智能体在多突变排序中表现突出，尤其在具备目标特异性适应度数据时。但随着搜索空间增大和突变深度增加，所有模型族均面临根本性挑战。

Conclusion: PFArena 揭示了不同模型范式在蛋白质改造决策中的互补优势与共同瓶颈，并公开代码与基准套件，以促进模型辅助蛋白质改造的可复现研究。

Abstract: Protein modification requires navigating an immense sequence space, yet wet-lab validation remains low-throughput and costly. Although computational paradigms including protein language models (PLMs), large language models (LLMs), and LLM-based agents have shown promise in protein modification, their relative efficacy across realistic experimental decision-making settings remains unclear. To bridge this gap, we introduce PFArena, a benchmark comprising four controlled task interfaces that cover single-mutant generation and multi-mutant ranking. By providing varying levels of mutation fitness data, PFArena reflects four representative research scenarios characterized by differing degrees of prior experimental context. We assess six PLMs, six LLMs, and five LLM-based agents using complementary metrics to measure both peak and overall protein modification performance. Our evaluation reveals that model performance shifts systematically with the availability of target-specific experimental evidence: PLMs demonstrate proficiency in open-ended single-mutant generation by leveraging protein-specific priors, whereas LLMs and agents perform strongly in multi-mutant ranking, particularly when target-specific fitness data are available. Nevertheless, all model families face fundamental challenges with increasing search-space size and mutation depth. We release our code and benchmark suite to facilitate reproducible research in model-assisted protein modification.

</details>


### [77] [When Should Forecasting Agents Reason? Behavioral Stress Tests for Reliability Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28475)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出ReliabilityRoute，将预测代理的检索、推理、市场先验与结构化类比选择视为可观测行为，发现机制优劣取决于数据来源，并用可靠性特征进行自适应路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测代理日益整合语言模型推理、检索、集成与校准，但人们不清楚何时应信任哪种行为；论文旨在回答机制选择何时可信、是否需要更多推理。

Method: 在ForecastBench风格二元预测任务上，将检索、推理、服从市场先验、使用历史类比作为可观测行为；提出ReliabilityRoute结构干预，利用历史覆盖率、市场先验可用性、来源先验锐度、证据强度、证据分歧与时间跨度等可靠性特征来引导代理行为；使用固定2024拟合规则与基于已结算版本的前向自适应阈值重拟合规则。

Result: 固定规则无需硬编码来源名称即可接近人工分类法；前向自适应规则在后续16个LLM版本上取得确定性系统中最优平均Brier分数。提升幅度有限，历史/搜索基线仍具高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是行为压力测试，表明更多推理并不总是更好；预测代理应先估计哪个证据源值得控制，路由策略本身也应在可审计约束下自适应，并提供可复现工件。

Abstract: Forecasting agents increasingly combine language-model reasoning, retrieval, ensembling, and calibration, but it remains unclear when each behavior should be trusted. We study this question on ForecastBench-style binary forecasting tasks, treating the choice to retrieve, reason, defer to a market prior, or use a historical analog as an observable agent behavior rather than a hidden implementation detail. Our central finding is that mechanism choice is source-dependent: structured analogs dominate for some data-generating processes, while market/crowd-style and conservative baselines are better for others. We introduce ReliabilityRoute, a structural intervention that steers forecasting-agent behavior using reliability features such as historical coverage, market-prior availability, source-prior sharpness, evidence strength, evidence disagreement, and horizon. A fixed 2024-fitted rule closely matches a hand taxonomy without hard-coded source-name decisions, while a walk-forward self-adjusting rule refits thresholds from previously resolved vintages and obtains the best mean Brier score among our deterministic systems across 16 later LLM vintages. The gain is modest and historical/search baselines remain highly competitive. The main contribution is therefore a behavioral stress test showing that more reasoning is not always better; forecasting agents should first estimate which evidence source deserves control, routing policies should themselves adapt under auditable constraints, and reproducibility artifacts are available at https://github.com/louiswang524/forcastagent

</details>


### [78] [TW3Cast: A Frozen Router of Lightly Fine-Tuned Foundation Models for Time-Series Forecasting on GIFT-Eval, Selected Entirely on the Training Split](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28506)
*Nathan Thierry,Andre-Louis Rochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TW3Cast 是一个不含智能体、不使用语言模型的时间序列预测系统，通过一张在训练集上一次性计算并冻结的路由表调用细调基础模型的专家，在 GIFT-Eval 榜单上以 19.4 平均 MASE 排名位列 130 个提交中的第 3 名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 榜单前两名属于依赖智能体或语言模型进行推理、生成与选择预测的 agentic 类别，成本高且复杂。作者希望验证：在完全不使用智能体与语言模型的前提下，能否用可复现、低成本、可控的方案达到接近榜首的竞争力。

Method: 系统核心是一张在训练划分上计算一次后即冻结的选择表，覆盖 97 个"数据集×频率×预测步长"配置，每个配置路由到四种模式之一：专家模型（对 Chronos-2、TiRex 或 Toto 做 LoRA 或全参数微调，训练数据经显式规则清洗与增强）、含专家的分位数混合、基础模型混合、或在训练集回测上进行的选拔锦标赛。所有决策均在训练回测上作出，专家一旦在该回测中胜过锦标赛即被接纳，候选成本仅为数 MB 与数分钟 GPU 时间。三重防护机制抑制选择偏差：精度与校准双重准则、针对训练期见过该序列的候选项的非对称裕度、逐窗口保守门槛；并通过时间元回测来确定选择规则本身。

Result: 单一最佳基础模型平均 MASE 排名 33.8，全配置启用锦标赛为 38.0，而完整路由表达到 19.4，显著优于两者。

Conclusion: 无需智能体与语言模型，仅靠冻结路由表与轻量微调专家即可在 GIFT-Eval 上取得第 3 名。作者公开路由表、专家索引、基础模型固定版本、提交分数文件及公开分数的时间戳快照，论文中全部榜单数字均可由一个脚本复现。

Abstract: TW3Cast is a time-series forecasting system that reaches position 3 of 130 entries on the GIFT-Eval benchmark by mean MASE rank, as of 2026-09-14. The two entries above it belong to the leaderboard's agentic category, multi-step systems that use agents or language models to reason about, generate or select forecasts. TW3Cast runs no agent and no language model. Its selection is a table computed once on the training split and then frozen, and its experts are public foundation models lightly fine-tuned on those training splits.
