TL;DR: 该论文提出AgentKernel，一个面向AI智能体的"信任原生"操作系统底座，通过内核级的强制不可绕过执行边界，将身份、感知、认知、执行四大支柱统一整合，从而在整个智能体生命周期内防御提示注入、记忆投毒、工具滥用等语义层攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI智能体频繁跨越信任边界：它们摄入不可信内容、与高权限指令结合、将中间信念持久化到长期记忆并调用高权限工具。这形成了攻击面——恶意载荷可经模型输入进入并触发有害的工具操作。然而当前治理栈仍是应用层中间件，与被监控的智能体共享同一进程信任边界，无法提供不可绕过的保障。因此作者主张智能体需要一个操作系统级底座，为身份、输入中介、记忆治理和执行控制提供强制性、不可绕过的服务。

Method: 作者设计并引入AgentKernel，将安全视为一等设计约束，把智能体生命周期包裹进一个强制性执行边界，并划分为四大支柱：Identity（身份）、Perception（感知）、Cognition（认知）、Execution（执行）。每个支柱将经典操作系统安全原则适配到语义层失效场景：内核托管身份支持可信的跨组织协作；渐进式感知替代脆弱的单点过滤；信息流受控记忆在提升检索保真度的同时抑制投毒；语义到内核的执行强制使得在高权限工具背后仍能维持不可绕过的边界。方法上通过系统性比较与安全分析论证该单一集成架构的有效性。

Result: AgentKernel被定位为位于编排框架、智能体运行时、治理平台与执行沙箱之下的"缺失的OS层"。系统比较与安全分析表明，该单一集成架构能够在完整智能体生命周期内实施安全防护，把结构性安全转化为能力倍增器：身份托管支撑可信跨组织协作，渐进感知提升鲁棒性，信息流控制记忆改善检索保真并限制投毒，语义到内核强制在不可绕过边界后开放更广的工具权限。

Conclusion: 智能体安全需要从应用层中间件上移为操作系统级底座，提供强制、不可绕过的身份、感知、认知与执行服务。AgentKernel以四支柱统一的信任原生架构，能够覆盖智能体全生命周期并应对委托滥用、提示注入、记忆投毒与工具误用等语义层失效，可作为现有编排与治理栈之下的基础OS层。

Abstract: Modern AI agents routinely cross trust boundaries: they ingest untrusted content, combine it with privileged instructions, persist intermediate beliefs in long-term memory, and invoke privileged tools. This creates an attack surface in which malicious payloads can enter through model inputs and cause harmful tool actions. Yet current governance stacks remain application-level middleware that share a process trust boundary with the agents they monitor. We argue that agents need an operating-system substrate providing mandatory, non-bypassable services for identity, input mediation, memory governance, and execution control.
  We introduce AgentKernel, a trust-native agent operating system built around the premise that security must be a first-class design constraint. AgentKernel wraps the agent lifecycle in a mandatory enforcement boundary organized into four pillars: Identity, Perception, Cognition, and Execution. Each pillar adapts classical OS security principles to failures at the semantic plane, including delegation abuse, prompt injection, memory poisoning, and tool misuse.
  AgentKernel treats structural security as a capability multiplier. Kernel-managed identity supports trustworthy cross-organization collaboration; graduated perception replaces brittle single-point filters; information-flow-controlled memory improves retrieval fidelity while limiting poisoning; and semantic-to-kernel enforcement permits broader tool privileges behind a non-bypassable boundary. We position AgentKernel as the missing OS layer beneath orchestration frameworks, agent runtimes, governance platforms, and execution sandboxes, and use systematic comparison and security analysis to show how a single integrated architecture can enforce security across the full agent lifecycle.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Reachability-Based Formal Verification of Graph Neural Networks with Node and Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30079)
*Anne M. Tumlin,Ben Wooding,Zhenxuan Shao,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Tyler Derr,Taylor T. Johnson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphStar集合扩展NNV框架，实现对GCN/GINE等GNN的鲁棒性验证，并在电力系统与图分类任务上验证其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN已成为电力系统快速、拓扑感知代理模型的重要方法，用于潮流分析、最优潮流估计和连锁故障分析等任务；但针对GNN模型的形式化验证仍然困难，现有方法适用范围有限。

Method: 通过GraphStar集合（Star集合的推广，可刻画节点与边特征上的不确定性）将神经网络安全验证框架扩展到图结构输入；支持线性消息传递操作的传播以及ReLU非线性的可靠近似，覆盖GCN层和带边特征的图同构网络（GINE）层。

Result: 在IEEE-24、IEEE-39、IEEE-118测试系统上的潮流、最优潮流与连锁故障三项电力任务，以及ENZYMES和PROTEINS两个图分类基准上评估GNNV；结果表明，对基于ReLU激活的图分类模型，GNNV比CORA提供更紧的鲁棒性保证；并首次在节点与边联合扰动下，为基于GINE的潮流与最优潮流模型提供边感知的鲁棒性保证。

Conclusion: GNNV将形式化验证扩展至图结构输入，能够为GCN/GINE类GNN提供更紧且具备边感知能力的鲁棒性保证，在电力系统代理模型验证与通用图分类任务中均展现有效性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have become a prominent approach for developing fast, topology-aware surrogates in electric power systems, supporting tasks such as power flow (PF) analysis, optimal power flow (OPF) estimation, and cascading failure analysis (CFA). Despite this growing use, formally verifying GNN-based models remains challenging, with existing methods limited in scope. We extend the neural network verification (NNV) framework to graph-structured inputs through GraphStar sets, a generalization of Star sets that captures uncertainty over both node and edge features. This extension enables the propagation of linear message-passing operations and the sound approximation of ReLU nonlinearities for GNN architectures, including graph convolutional network (GCN) and graph isomorphism network with edge features (GINE) layers. We evaluate GNNV across three power system tasks, PF, OPF, and CFA, on the IEEE-24, IEEE-39, and IEEE-118 test cases, as well as two standard graph classification benchmarks, ENZYMES and PROTEINS. Our results show that GNNV provides tighter robustness guarantees than CORA on graph classification models with ReLU-based activations and, for the first time, delivers edge-aware robustness guarantees for GINE-based PF and OPF models under joint node and edge perturbations.

</details>


### [146] [Learning to Discover Interesting Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28603)
*Niket Patel,Ahmad Rammal,Amaury Hayat,Remi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用"证明长度/陈述长度"定义定理的内在有趣性，并训练难度预测模型，指导LLM自动生成更有价值、更新颖的定理，构建自扩展的形式化数学库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已能证明大量甚至长期未解的数学问题，但机器生成的新定理是否"有趣、有用"缺乏判定标准；且依赖人工给定目标，难以实现规模化、自动化的数学知识扩展。

Method: 定义定理的内在有趣性为证明长度与陈述长度之比；验证其与下游效用的外在度量强相关；以"在给定前提集下证明的难度"为核心计算原语，训练27B规模的证明难度预测模型；以该指标进行优化与候选定理筛选，并在自扩展数学库中迭代累积。

Result: 该27B模型预测证明难度比前沿通用模型更准确；按该指标优化后模型能产出更有趣的定理，与Mathlib的重叠率由91.9%降至30.6%，生成更多分布外数学内容；系统可完成生成—筛选—迭代扩展的闭环。

Conclusion: 该指标为猜想排序与证明搜索提供了可量化信号，指向一种不依赖人工目标、可自主选择有价值命题并持续扩展的机器可验证数学库。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly able to solve advanced mathematical problems, including many that have been open for decades. This opens the door to expansion of mathematical knowledge at unprecedented scale. Yet, while LLMs may be able to conjecture and prove more and more theorems, it remains open whether this new mathematical knowledge is interesting or useful. We define intrinsic interestingness of a theorem as the ratio between the length of its proof and the length of its statement. We show that this correlates strongly with an extrinsic measure of the downstream utility of a theorem. We identify the difficulty of a proof conditioned on a set of premises as a useful primitive for computing these metrics, and train a 27B model that predicts proof difficulty more accurately than frontier general-purpose models. Optimizing for our metric creates a model capable of producing more interesting theorems, while also reducing substantial or full overlap with Mathlib from 91.9% to 30.6%, showcasing the creation of more out-of-distribution math. We show that our system can generate candidate theorems, select the most interesting among them, and iteratively build on a self-expanding mathematical library. These metrics provide a practical and quantifiable signal for ranking conjectures and guiding proof search within formal mathematical libraries. Our framework provides a path towards self-expanding, machine-verified mathematical libraries that can choose worthwhile statements without relying on human-supplied targets.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Representation World Model: Learning States, Transition and Executable Plans in Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29171)
*Yijun Yuan,Weicheng Zheng,Weibang Wang,Minghui Qin,Chang Sun,Junhao Huang,Kenan Li,Anmin Liu,Yicheng Yao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Representation World Model (RWM)，直接在表征空间中学习状态、转移与可执行规划，避免递归rollout与动作空间搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常将潜在表征与显式动力学模型联合学习，并通过搜索、优化或基于策略的预测进行规划，计算开销大且规划过程与表征学习分离。作者希望将规划直接融入表征几何，探索表征空间规划作为传统世界模型规划的替代方案。

Method: RWM直接在表征空间中学习状态、转移和规划。其核心是：利用由端点表征构造的潜在路径，沿路径局部施加逆动力学监督来学习表征几何，要求这些路径保留任务相关的状态与转移信息。推理时，直接在当前表征与目标表征之间构造潜在路径，再用逆动力学恢复对应动作，无需递归rollout或动作空间搜索。

Result: 在连续控制基准上验证了RWM用于直接规划的有效性；在机器人操作任务上的结果进一步表明其具备扩展到更复杂具身控制任务的潜力。

Conclusion: 在表征空间中直接规划为传统世界模型规划提供了一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: We propose the Representation World Model (RWM), which learns states, transitions, and executable plans directly in representation space. Unlike existing world models that typically learn latent representations together with explicit dynamics models and perform planning through search, optimization, or policy-based prediction, RWM directly incorporates planning into the learned representation geometry. RWM learns the representation geometry by applying inverse-dynamics supervision locally along latent paths constructed from endpoint representations, requiring these paths to preserve task-relevant state and transition information. At inference, planning is performed by directly constructing a latent path between the current and goal representations, with inverse dynamics used to recover the corresponding actions, without recursive rollouts or action-space search. Experiments on continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of RWM for direct planning, while results on robotic manipulation further show its potential to extend to more complex embodied control tasks. These results suggest that planning directly in representation space provides a promising alternative to conventional world-model planning.

</details>


### [148] [RAPID: Robot Agentic Programming from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30249)
*Yuyao Liu,Jiayuan Mao,David Hsu,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Tomás Lozano-Pérez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAPID，一种从单次视觉人类演示中自动生成、验证并精化机器人程序的编码智能体框架，通过对象中心关系型程序表示实现跨物体位姿、形状、材质与环境的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在复杂编程任务上取得成功，但将其潜力迁移到机器人系统存在挑战。现有方法依赖特定运动轨迹，难以泛化到演示之外的新场景。作者希望借助智能体式代码生成与迭代精化的能力，从单次演示中自动获得可复用、可泛化的机器人操作程序。

Method: RAPID构建迭代式智能体循环进行代码精化，其核心依赖于从演示中自动推断的三要素：(i) 可测试的任务规范，(ii) 机器人执行的动作原语，(iii) 用于执行与验证的交互式环境。为提升可复用性，RAPID采用对象中心的关系型程序表示，关注演示策略的底层结构而非具体运动：将动作原语表达为实现物体级运动效果的轨迹优化程序，并在运行时通过关系约束组合这些原语，以刻画场景特定几何。

Result: 在仿真中，RAPID在八个具有挑战性的富接触非抓取操作任务以及LIBERO-Pro基准中的通用抓取操作任务上进行了评估；同时成功部署于真实Franka机械臂并在全部八个非抓取任务上测试。所有实验中RAPID均表现强劲，并在物体位姿、形状、材质和环境方面展现出泛化能力。

Conclusion: RAPID表明，将编码智能体的自动程序生成与精化范式引入机器人领域，配合对象中心关系型表示，可从单次人类演示中获得可测试、可执行、可泛化的机器人操作程序，在仿真与真实平台上均验证了其有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Coding agents have demonstrated enormous success in solving complex programming problems. To leverage their potential for robot systems, this work introduces Robot Agentic Programming from Demonstrations (RAPID), which automatically generates, verifies, and refines robot programs, given a single visual human demonstration. The iterative agentic loop of code refinement requires several key ingredients: (i) a testable task specification, (ii) action primitives for robot execution, and (iii) an interactive environment for program execution and verification. RAPID infers all three from the demonstration automatically. To make the resulting program reusable beyond the demonstration setting, RAPID uses an object-centric relational program representation that focuses on the underlying structure of the demonstrated strategy rather than the specific motion per se: it expresses the action primitives as trajectory-optimization programs that realize object-level motion effects, while composing them through relational constraints that capture scene-specific geometry at run time. We evaluated RAPID in simulation on eight challenging contact-rich nonprehensile manipulation tasks as well as general prehensile manipulation tasks in the LIBERO-Pro benchmark. We also successfully deployed it on a real Franka arm and evaluated on all eight nonprehensile tasks. In all experiments, RAPID demonstrated strong performance, with generalization over object pose, shape, material, and environment. Website: https://yuyaoliu.me/projects/rapid.

</details>


### [149] [World Action Agent: Harnessing VLMs for Robot Manipulation via World Action Rehearsal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29964)
*Yehang Zhang,Haojian Huang,Yifan Chang,Jianchong Su,Bohan Zhou,Yingjie Xu,Wosong Chen,Tianhao Zhou,Chenxu Wang,Tianyi Zhang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Shiyuan Deng,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen,Zexi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 WAA（World Action Agent），一种多智能体框架，让 VLM 通过基础工具在视觉动作工作区中直接驱动机器人，并在 LIBERO-Pro 上达到 75.6% 平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型（VLM）具备广泛知识与空间推理能力，但现有机器人操作系统要么仅间接使用 VLM（预测约束或编写程序），要么只给 VLM 一个场景视图而非可行动的世界，限制了其直接参与操作决策的能力。

Method: WAA 构建视觉动作工作区，包含三项特性：(1) Contact views——根据场景几何自动选取接触区域视图；(2) Action rehearsal——将每个动作转为可编辑提案，由智能体自身或 Imagination Agent 依据规划反馈预览与修改；(3) In-view correction——在观测视图中闭环修正残余偏移。此外，通过 Evidence-based review 从专家视频与人类教学中演化多模态技能并由 Skill Agent 调用；交互轨迹用于训练更小 VLM 驱动同一框架。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro 上，仅用 LIBERO-90 演化技能的 WAA 达到 75.6% 平均成功率，优于端到端 VLA、code-as-policy 智能体及相同骨干的 visual-harness 基线；技能在 robosuite 上无需额外学习仍有效。用 harness 轨迹微调 Qwen3.5-9B 后，其域外成功率从 1.7% 提升至 43.3%。

Conclusion: WAA 证明 VLM 可直接作为机器人操作的主体，通过视觉动作工作区实现感知—预演—执行闭环，并能通过技能演化与轨迹蒸馏实现具身程序性知识的获取与迁移。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) bring broad knowledge and spatial reasoning to robot manipulation, yet existing systems either use them indirectly, to predict constraints or write programs, or give them a view of the scene rather than a world in which to act. We present World Action Agent (WAA), a multi-agent harness through which VLMs pilot robots with basic tools, making every decision within a visual action workspace. The workspace has three properties. Contact views, selected automatically from the scene geometry, present the scene around the current interaction. Action rehearsal turns each action into an editable proposal that the agent, alone or through an Imagination Agent, previews and revises against planning feedback before execution. In-view correction closes the loop between observation, rehearsal, and low-level execution, letting the agent remove residual offsets in the view where it observes them. Through the same workspace, WAA acquires embodied procedural knowledge in two ways: it evolves multimodal skills from expert videos and human teaching under evidence-based review and consults them through a Skill Agent, and its interaction traces train smaller VLMs to pilot the same harness. On LIBERO-Pro, WAA with skills evolved only from LIBERO-90 reaches a state-of-the-art 75.6% average success, outperforming end-to-end VLAs, code-as-policy agents, and a visual-harness baseline with the same backbone; the same skills remain effective on robosuite without further learning. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on harness traces raises its out-of-domain success from 1.7% to 43.3%.

</details>


### [150] [Coding Agents for Generalized Task and Motion Planning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30233)
*Matteo Merler,Bowen Li,Josh Roy,Yichao Liang,Qianwei Wang,Yixuan Huang,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 编码智能体可通过合成可泛化程序自动化广义TAMP，性能超过手工规划器与一次性生成及LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义TAMP需利用问题实例间的规律性以降低新实例规划开销，但现有方法依赖大量TAMP专用工程，故探究编码智能体能否自动化该过程。

Method: 给定任务描述与仿真器访问权限，各智能体在固定合成预算内自主选择环境交互方式并开发程序，随后冻结程序并在未见实例上评估；在KinDER与PDDLStream的28个仿真环境（对象数超出原基准）中评估Claude Code(Opus 5)与Codex(GPT-5.6 Sol、GPT-6 Astra)，共980个程序、每程序100个留出实例、总计98,000个评估回合。

Result: 三种智能体配置在平均成功率上均超过手工规划器、一次性生成及LLM广义规划基线（56%–95% 对规划器的47%，基于可提供规划器的16个环境）；随对象数增长，智能体程序成功率仍高于规划器，且平均每实例计算量低一个数量级；日志显示智能体利用交互校准物理模型、测试边界情形并改进策略。

Conclusion: 编码智能体是广义TAMP的强基线，作者公开全部代码与完整提示词。

Abstract: Task and motion planning (TAMP) problems remain difficult even with full observability and object-centric states because discrete decisions are tightly coupled to geometric, kinematic, and dynamic constraints. Generalized TAMP addresses this difficulty by exploiting regularities across problem instances to reduce planning effort on new instances. However, existing methods require substantial TAMP-specific engineering. We investigate whether coding agents can automate this process by synthesizing programs that generalize across instances. Given a task description and simulator access, each agent chooses how to interact with the environment while developing a program within a fixed synthesis budget. The program is then frozen and evaluated on unseen instances. We evaluate Claude Code (Opus 5) and Codex (GPT-5.6 Sol and GPT-6 Astra) on 28 simulated environments from KinDER and PDDLStream, with object counts beyond those evaluated in the original benchmark. Across all program synthesis methods, we evaluate 980 generated programs on 100 held-out instances each, 98,000 evaluation episodes in total. Overall, we find that coding agents are surprisingly effective at generalized TAMP: all three agent configurations outperform hand-engineered planners, one-shot generation, and an LLM-based generalized planning baseline in mean success (56% to 95% versus 47% for the planners, on the 16 environments where a planner is available). As object counts grow, the agents' programs maintain higher success than the planner, using an order of magnitude less computation per instance on average. Logs show agents using interaction to calibrate physical models, test edge cases, and refine strategies. We release all code, including the full prompts given to the agents. These findings suggest that coding agents are a strong baseline for generalized TAMP.

</details>
